import {

  useEffect,

  useState,
} from "react";

  getPrivacyGovernanceCase,
  getPrivacyGovernanceCases,
  getPrivacyGovernanceStatusChart,
  getPrivacyGovernanceSummary,
} from "../../lib/privacy-governance/api";

import type {
  PrivacyGovernanceCase,
  PrivacyGovernanceCaseDetail,
  PrivacyGovernanceStatusItem,
  PrivacyGovernanceSummary,
} from "../../lib/privacy-governance/types";

import PrivacyGovernanceCaseDetailComponent from "./PrivacyGovernanceCaseDetail";
import styles from "./PrivacyGovernanceDashboard.module.css";

interface Props {
  initialCaseId?: string | null;
}

type CaseFilter =
  | "ALL"
  | "PENDING_DPO_GRC"
  | "APPROVED"
  | "REJECTED"
  | "BLOCKED";

function statusLabel(
  value?: string | null,
): string {
  if (!value) {
    return "Unknown";
  }

  return value.replaceAll("_", " ");
}

function formatDate(
  value?: string | null,
): string {
  if (!value) {
    return "—";
  }

  const date = new Date(value);

  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
    return value;
  }

  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined, {
    dateStyle: "medium",
    timeStyle: "short",
  }).format(date);
}

function isMatchingFilter(
  caseItem: PrivacyGovernanceCase,
  filter: CaseFilter,
): boolean {
  if (filter === "ALL") {
    return true;
  }

  return (
    caseItem.governance_status === filter
  );
}

function getApprovalHistory(
  caseData: PrivacyGovernanceCase | null,
) {
  if (!caseData) {
    return [];
  }

  return caseData.approval_history ?? [];
}

/**
 * Privacy Governance Dashboard
 *
 * Responsibilities:
 * - Load dashboard summary data.
 * - Load dashboard status distribution.
 * - Load lightweight case register data.
 * - Load authoritative case detail only when a case is opened.
 * - Apply client-side filtering to the case register.
 * - Keep case-detail lifecycle separate from dashboard lifecycle.
 *
 * The component deliberately does not require React Router.
 * This makes the dashboard independently testable and reusable.
 */
export function PrivacyGovernanceDashboard({
  initialCaseId,
}: Props) {
  const [summary, setSummary] =
    useState<PrivacyGovernanceSummary | null>(
      null,
    );

  const [statusChart, setStatusChart] =
    useState<PrivacyGovernanceStatusItem[]>(
      [],
    );

  const [cases, setCases] = useState<
    PrivacyGovernanceCase[]
  >([]);

  const [selectedCaseId, setSelectedCaseId] =
    useState<string | null>(
      initialCaseId ?? null,
    );

  const [selectedCase, setSelectedCase] =
    useState<PrivacyGovernanceCaseDetail | null>(
      null,
    );

  const [
    caseDetailLoading,
    setCaseDetailLoading,
  ] = useState(false);

  const [caseDetailError, setCaseDetailError] =
    useState<string | null>(null);

  const [filter, setFilter] =
    useState<CaseFilter>("ALL");

  const [loading, setLoading] =
    useState(true);

  const [error, setError] =
    useState<string | null>(null);

  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] =
    useState(false);

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  /* Dashboard loading                                                       */
  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------ */

  const loadDashboard = useCallback(
    async (isRefresh = false) => {
      if (isRefresh) {
        setRefreshing(true);
      } else {
        setLoading(true);
      }

      setError(null);

      try {
        const [
          summaryResult,
          statusChartResult,
          casesResult,
        ] = await Promise.all([
          getPrivacyGovernanceSummary(),
          getPrivacyGovernanceStatusChart(),
          getPrivacyGovernanceCases(),
        ]);

        setSummary(summaryResult);
        setStatusChart(statusChartResult);
        setCases(casesResult);
      } catch (cause) {
        setError(
          cause instanceof Error
            ? cause.message
            : "Unable to load privacy governance dashboard.",
        );
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
        setRefreshing(false);
      }
    },
    [],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    void loadDashboard();
  }, [loadDashboard]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!initialCaseId) {
      return;
    }

    setSelectedCaseId(initialCaseId);
  }, [initialCaseId]);

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  /* Selected case detail                                                     */
  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------ */

  const loadCaseDetail = useCallback(
    async (caseId: string) => {
      setCaseDetailLoading(true);
      setCaseDetailError(null);
      setSelectedCase(null);

      try {
        const detail =
          await getPrivacyGovernanceCase(
            caseId,
          );

        setSelectedCase(detail);
      } catch (cause) {
        setCaseDetailError(
          cause instanceof Error
            ? cause.message
            : "Unable to load privacy governance case.",
        );
      } finally {
        setCaseDetailLoading(false);
      }
    },
    [],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!selectedCaseId) {
      setSelectedCase(null);
      setCaseDetailError(null);
      setCaseDetailLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    void loadCaseDetail(selectedCaseId);
  }, [
    selectedCaseId,
    loadCaseDetail,
  ]);

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  /* Filtering                                                                */
  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------ */

  const filteredCases = useMemo(
    () =>
      cases.filter((caseItem) =>
        isMatchingFilter(
          caseItem,
          filter,
        ),
      ),
    [cases, filter],
  );

  const applyFilter = useCallback(
    (nextFilter: CaseFilter) => {
      setFilter((current) =>
        current === nextFilter
          ? "ALL"
          : nextFilter,
      );
    },
    [],
  );

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  /* Case drawer                                                              */
  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------ */

  const openCase = useCallback(
    (caseId: string) => {
      setSelectedCaseId(caseId);
    },
    [],
  );

  const closeCase = useCallback(() => {
    setSelectedCaseId(null);
    setSelectedCase(null);
    setCaseDetailError(null);
  }, []);

  const retryCaseDetail = useCallback(() => {
    if (!selectedCaseId) {
      return;
    }

    void loadCaseDetail(selectedCaseId);
  }, [
    selectedCaseId,
    loadCaseDetail,
  ]);

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  /* KPI values                                                               */
  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------ */

  const pendingCount =
    summary?.pending ??
    cases.filter(
      (caseItem) =>
        caseItem.governance_status ===
        "PENDING_DPO_GRC",
    ).length;

  const approvedCount =
    summary?.approved ??
    cases.filter(
      (caseItem) =>
        caseItem.governance_status ===
        "APPROVED",
    ).length;

  const blockedCount =
    summary?.blocked ??
    cases.filter(
      (caseItem) =>
        caseItem.governance_status ===
        "BLOCKED",
    ).length;

  const totalCount =
    summary?.total_cases ??
    cases.length;

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  /* Initial loading                                                          */
  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------ */

  if (loading && !summary) {
    return (
      <section
        className={styles.dashboard}
        aria-label="Privacy governance dashboard"
      >
        <div
          className={styles.loadingState}
          role="status"
        >
          Loading privacy governance dashboard…
        </div>
      </section>
    );
  }

  return (
    <section
      className={styles.dashboard}
      aria-label="Privacy governance dashboard"
    >
      {/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */}
      {/* Header                                                             */}
      {/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */}

      <header className={styles.header}>
        <div>
          <p className={styles.eyebrow}>
            PRIVACY GOVERNANCE
          </p>

          <h1>
            Governance Control Center
          </h1>

          <p className={styles.description}>
            Monitor privacy change governance,
            review approval evidence, and
            record controlled human decisions.
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() =>
            void loadDashboard(true)
          }
          disabled={refreshing}
          className={styles.refreshButton}
        >
          {refreshing
            ? "Refreshing…"
            : "Refresh"}
        </button>
      </header>

      {/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */}
      {/* Dashboard error                                                     */}
      {/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */}

      {error && (
        <div
          className={styles.errorState}
          role="alert"
        >
          <strong>
            Unable to load dashboard data.
          </strong>

          <span>{error}</span>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() =>
              void loadDashboard()
            }
          >
            Retry
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */}
      {/* KPI cards                                                           */}
      {/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */}

      <section
        className={styles.kpiGrid}
        aria-label="Privacy governance KPIs"
      >
        <button
          type="button"
          className={styles.kpiCard}
          aria-label="Filter all cases"
          onClick={() =>
            applyFilter("ALL")
          }
        >
          <span>Total cases</span>
          <strong>{totalCount}</strong>
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          className={styles.kpiCard}
          aria-label="Filter pending DPO/GRC cases"
          onClick={() =>
            applyFilter(
              "PENDING_DPO_GRC",
            )
          }
        >
          <span>Pending DPO/GRC</span>
          <strong>{pendingCount}</strong>
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          className={styles.kpiCard}
          aria-label="Filter approved cases"
          onClick={() =>
            applyFilter("APPROVED")
          }
        >
          <span>Approved</span>
          <strong>{approvedCount}</strong>
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          className={styles.kpiCard}
          aria-label="Filter blocked cases"
          onClick={() =>
            applyFilter("BLOCKED")
          }
        >
          <span>Blocked</span>
          <strong>{blockedCount}</strong>
        </button>
      </section>

      {/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */}
      {/* Status chart                                                        */}
      {/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */}

      {statusChart.length > 0 && (
        <section
          className={styles.section}
          aria-label="Governance status chart"
        >
          <div
            className={
              styles.sectionHeading
            }
          >
            <div>
              <p
                className={styles.eyebrow}
              >
                STATUS DISTRIBUTION
              </p>

              <h2>
                Governance status
              </h2>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div
            className={styles.statusChart}
          >
            {statusChart.map(
              (status) => (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  key={status.status}
                  className={
                    styles.statusItem
                  }
                  aria-label={`${status.status} ${status.count}`}
                  onClick={() =>
                    applyFilter(
                      status.status as CaseFilter,
                    )
                  }
                >
                  <span>
                    {statusLabel(
                      status.status,
                    )}
                  </span>

                  <strong>
                    {status.count}
                  </strong>
                </button>
              ),
            )}
          </div>
        </section>
      )}

      {/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */}
      {/* Case register                                                       */}
      {/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */}

      <section
        className={styles.section}
        aria-label="Privacy governance cases"
      >
        <div
          className={styles.sectionHeading}
        >
          <div>
            <p
              className={styles.eyebrow}
            >
              CASE REGISTER
            </p>

            <h2>
              Governance cases
            </h2>
          </div>

          <span>
            {filteredCases.length} case
            {filteredCases.length === 1
              ? ""
              : "s"}
          </span>
        </div>

        {filteredCases.length === 0 ? (
          <div
            className={styles.emptyState}
          >
            No governance cases match the
            selected filter.
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div
            className={styles.caseList}
          >
            {filteredCases.map(
              (caseItem) => (
                <article
                  key={caseItem.case_id}
                  className={
                    styles.caseCard
                  }
                >
                  <div
                    className={
                      styles.caseCardHeader
                    }
                  >
                    <div>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        className={
                          styles.caseId
                        }
                        aria-label={
                          caseItem.case_id
                        }
                        onClick={() =>
                          openCase(
                            caseItem.case_id,
                          )
                        }
                      >
                        {caseItem.case_id}
                      </button>

                      <h3>
                        {caseItem.title}
                      </h3>
                    </div>

                    <span
                      className={
                        styles.statusBadge
                      }
                    >
                      {statusLabel(
                        caseItem.governance_status,
                      )}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <p
                    className={
                      styles.caseSummary
                    }
                  >
                    {caseItem.summary}
                  </p>

                  <dl
                    className={
                      styles.caseMetadata
                    }
                  >
                    <div>
                      <dt>Risk</dt>
                      <dd>
                        {statusLabel(
                          caseItem.risk,
                        )}
                      </dd>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <dt>Validation</dt>
                      <dd>
                        {statusLabel(
                          caseItem.validation_status,
                        )}
                      </dd>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <dt>Decision</dt>
                      <dd>
                        {statusLabel(
                          caseItem.final_decision,
                        )}
                      </dd>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <dt>Updated</dt>
                      <dd>
                        {formatDate(
                          caseItem.final_decision_at,
                        )}
                      </dd>
                    </div>
                  </dl>

                  <div
                    className={
                      styles.caseActions
                    }
                  >
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      aria-label={`View case ${caseItem.case_id}`}
                      onClick={() =>
                        openCase(
                          caseItem.case_id,
                        )
                      }
                    >
                      View case
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </article>
              ),
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </section>

      {/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */}
      {/* Case detail drawer                                                  */}
      /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */}

      <PrivacyGovernanceCaseDetailComponent
        open={Boolean(selectedCaseId)}
        loading={caseDetailLoading}
        error={caseDetailError}
        caseData={selectedCase}
        onClose={closeCase}
        onRetry={retryCaseDetail}
        onDecisionComplete={(
          updatedCase,
        ) => {
          setSelectedCase(updatedCase);

          setCases((current) =>
            current.map(
              (caseItem) =>
                caseItem.case_id ===
                updatedCase.case_id
                  ? {
                      ...caseItem,
                      governance_status:
                        updatedCase.governance_status,
                      final_decision:
                        updatedCase.final_decision,
                      final_decision_at:
                        updatedCase.final_decision_at,
                    }
                  : caseItem,
            ),
          );

          setSummary((current) => {
            if (!current) {
              return current;
            }

            return current;
          });
        }}
      />
    </section>
  );
}

export default PrivacyGovernanceDashboard;

export interface PrivacyGovernanceApprovalRecord {
  approval_id: string;
  case_id: string;
  change_id: string;
  reviewer: string;
  role: string;
  decision: string;
  comment: string;
  decided_at?: string | null;
}

export interface PrivacyGovernanceCaseTrigger {
  type: string;
  at: string;
  source: string;
}

export interface PrivacyGovernanceCaseDetail {
  /**
   * Stable governance-case identifier.
   *
   * Example:
   * CASE-E2E-003
   */
  case_id: string;

  /**
   * Authoritative Privacy Change Registry identifier.
   *
   * Example:
   * PCR-E2E-001
   */
  change_id: string;

  /**
   * Human-readable assessment title.
   */
  title: string;

  /**
   * Concise authoritative assessment summary.
   */
  summary: string;

  /**
   * Authoritative trigger information.
   *
   * The backend exposes this as a nested object rather
   * than flattened trigger_type / trigger_source /
   * triggered_at properties.
   */
  trigger: PrivacyGovernanceCaseTrigger;

  /**
   * Governance risk classification.
   */
  risk: string;

  /**
   * Authoritative processing identifiers associated
   * with the governance case.
   */
  processing_ids: string[];

  /**
   * Authoritative data-mapping identifiers associated
   * with the governance case.
   */
  data_map_ids: string[];

  /**
   * Result of automated privacy validation.
   */
  validation_status: string;

  /**
   * Email delivery status.
   */
  email_status: string;

  /**
   * Current governance lifecycle status.
   *
   * Pending:
   *   PENDING_DPO_GRC
   *
   * Completed states:
   *   APPROVED
   *   REJECTED
   *   BLOCKED
   */
  governance_status: string;

  /**
   * Final governance decision.
   *
   * Null while the case remains pending.
   */
  final_decision: string | null;

  /**
   * Timestamp of the final governance decision.
   *
   * Null while the case remains pending.
   */
  final_decision_at: string | null;

  /**
   * Authoritative governance approval/decision evidence.
   */
  approval_history: PrivacyGovernanceApprovalRecord[];
}

export type PrivacyGovernanceCase =
  PrivacyGovernanceCaseDetail;

export type GovernanceFilter =
  | "ALL"
  | "PENDING_DPO_GRC"
  | "APPROVED"
  | "REJECTED"
  | "BLOCKED";

export interface PrivacyGovernanceSummary {
  total_cases: number;
  pending: number;
  approved: number;
  blocked: number;
  approval_records: number;
}

export interface PrivacyGovernanceStatusItem {
  status: string;
  count: number;
}

/*
 * =========================================================
 * EasyChatBot Main Dashboard Application
 * =========================================================
 *
 * The Home dashboard does NOT allow the learner to select
 * an AI companion.
 *
 * The learner already has a companion.
 *
 * The flow is:
 *
 *     Authenticated Learner
 *             +
 *       Existing Companion
 *             +
 *            World
 *             +
 *         Languages
 *             ↓
 *       Dashboard Context
 *             ↓
 *       Character Theme API
 *             ↓
 *       Resolved Dashboard Theme
 *
 *
 * Character identity is therefore NOT controlled by the
 * Home page.
 *
 * The current development learner context is supplied by:
 *
 *     window.EasyChatBotLearner
 *
 * from index.html.
 *
 * Later this object will be replaced by the authenticated
 * learner/session API.
 *
 *
 * Responsibilities of this file:
 *
 * - initialise the Home dashboard;
 * - read the learner's existing companion;
 * - load companion information;
 * - request the resolved character theme;
 * - populate companion presentation;
 * - handle dashboard actions;
 * - send chat messages;
 * - preserve separation between:
 *
 *       Character identity
 *       Theme resolution
 *       Dashboard presentation
 *       Chat execution
 *
 * Character themes are NOT defined here.
 *
 * Theme resolution remains a backend responsibility.
 *
 * =========================================================
 */


/*
 * =========================================================
 * Global Dashboard State
 * =========================================================
 */


/*
 * Technical character identifier.
 *
 * Example:
 *
 *     "luminate"
 *     "yeti"
 *     "clara"
 *     "layla"
 *
 * This is NOT selected by the Home UI.
 */

let characterId = null;


/*
 * Character display name.
 *
 * Example:
 *
 *     "Luminate"
 */

let character = null;


/*
 * Resolved character profile from characters.js.
 */

let activeCharacter = null;


/*
 * Current learner context.
 */

let learnerContext = null;


/*
 * Current resolved dashboard theme.
 */

let activeDashboardTheme = null;


/*
 * =========================================================
 * Development / Session Context
 * =========================================================
 *
 * index.html currently provides:
 *
 *     window.EasyChatBotLearner
 *
 * Example:
 *
 * {
 *     characterId: "luminate",
 *     worldId: "france",
 *     primaryLanguage: "fr",
 *     secondaryLanguage: "es"
 * }
 *
 *
 * This is deliberately a context object rather than a
 * character selector.
 *
 * =========================================================
 */


function getLearnerContext() {

    const context =
        window.EasyChatBotLearner;


    if (!context) {

        console.error(
            "EasyChatBot learner context was not found."
        );

        return null;

    }


    return {

        characterId:
            context.characterId || null,

        worldId:
            context.worldId || "france",

        primaryLanguage:
            context.primaryLanguage || "fr",

        secondaryLanguage:
            context.secondaryLanguage || null

    };

}


/*
 * =========================================================
 * Character Lookup
 * =========================================================
 *
 * characters.js remains the frontend character metadata
 * source for presentation copy.
 *
 * It is NOT used to select a character.
 *
 * The learner's character ID comes from the learner context.
 *
 * =========================================================
 */


function getCharacter(
    requestedCharacterId
) {

    if (
        typeof characters ===
        "undefined"
    ) {

        console.error(
            "characters.js was not loaded."
        );

        return null;

    }


    if (
        !Array.isArray(characters)
    ) {

        console.error(
            "The characters collection is invalid."
        );

        return null;

    }


    return characters.find(
        function (item) {

            return (
                item.id ===
                requestedCharacterId
            );

        }
    ) || null;

}


/*
 * =========================================================
 * Dashboard Initialisation
 * =========================================================
 */


async function initialiseDashboard() {

    console.log(
        "EasyChatBot Home dashboard initialising..."
    );


    /*
     * -----------------------------------------------------
     * Read learner context
     * -----------------------------------------------------
     */

    learnerContext =
        getLearnerContext();


    if (!learnerContext) {

        showDashboardError(
            "Learner context is unavailable."
        );

        return;

    }


    /*
     * -----------------------------------------------------
     * Validate existing companion
     * -----------------------------------------------------
     */

    characterId =
        learnerContext.characterId;


    if (!characterId) {

        showDashboardError(
            "No companion is assigned to this learner."
        );

        return;

    }


    /*
     * -----------------------------------------------------
     * Load character metadata
     * -----------------------------------------------------
     */

    activeCharacter =
        getCharacter(
            characterId
        );


    if (!activeCharacter) {

        console.error(
            "Character metadata not found:",
            characterId
        );


        showDashboardError(
            "Your companion could not be loaded."
        );

        return;

    }


    character =
        activeCharacter.name;


    console.log(
        "Existing learner companion:",
        character
    );


    console.log(
        "Character ID:",
        characterId
    );


    /*
     * -----------------------------------------------------
     * Populate dashboard companion presentation
     * -----------------------------------------------------
     */

    updateCompanionPresentation(
        activeCharacter
    );


    /*
     * -----------------------------------------------------
     * Load character/world theme
     * -----------------------------------------------------
     */

    await loadCharacterTheme();


    /*
     * -----------------------------------------------------
     * Initialise dashboard interactions
     * -----------------------------------------------------
     */

    initialiseNavigation();

    initialiseDashboardActions();


    console.log(
        "EasyChatBot Home dashboard ready."
    );

}


/*
 * =========================================================
 * Companion Presentation
 * =========================================================
 *
 * This function updates the Home dashboard with the
 * companion that already belongs to the learner.
 *
 * It does NOT create a character-selection UI.
 *
 * =========================================================
 */


function updateCompanionPresentation(
    selected
) {

    if (!selected) {

        return;

    }


    /*
     * -----------------------------------------------------
     * Companion name
     * -----------------------------------------------------
     */

    setText(
        "companion-name-pick",
        selected.name
    );


    /*
     * -----------------------------------------------------
     * Hero title
     * -----------------------------------------------------
     */

    const heroTitle =
        document.getElementById(
            "companion-title"
        );


    if (heroTitle) {

        heroTitle.textContent =
            selected.name +
            " is ready for you!";

    }


    /*
     * -----------------------------------------------------
     * Hero message
     * -----------------------------------------------------
     */

    const heroMessage =
        document.getElementById(
            "companion-message"
        );


    if (heroMessage) {

        heroMessage.textContent =
            getCompanionMessage(
                selected
            );

    }


    /*
     * -----------------------------------------------------
     * Companion quote
     * -----------------------------------------------------
     */

    const quote =
        document.getElementById(
            "companion-quote"
        );


    if (quote) {

        quote.textContent =
            getCompanionQuote(
                selected
            );

    }


    /*
     * -----------------------------------------------------
     * Quote author
     * -----------------------------------------------------
     */

    setText(
        "companion-quote-author",
        "— " + selected.name
    );


    /*
     * -----------------------------------------------------
     * Companion pick message
     * -----------------------------------------------------
     */

    const pickMessage =
        document.getElementById(
            "pick-message"
        );


    if (pickMessage) {

        pickMessage.textContent =
            getCompanionPickMessage(
                selected
            );

    }


    /*
     * -----------------------------------------------------
     * Character visual
     * -----------------------------------------------------
     */

    updateCompanionVisual(
        selected
    );


    /*
     * -----------------------------------------------------
     * HTML metadata
     * -----------------------------------------------------
     */

    document.documentElement.dataset.character =
        selected.id;


}


/*
 * =========================================================
 * Companion Messaging
 * =========================================================
 */


function getCompanionMessage(
    selected
) {

    if (
        selected.recommendation &&
        selected.recommendation.tone
    ) {

        return (
            "Your " +
            selected.name +
            " adventure continues. " +
            "Let's learn together."
        );

    }


    return (
        "Your learning adventure with " +
        selected.name +
        " continues."
    );

}


/*
 * =========================================================
 * Companion Quote
 * =========================================================
 */


function getCompanionQuote(
    selected
) {

    if (
        selected.recommendation &&
        selected.recommendation.openingPhrase
    ) {

        return selected
            .recommendation
            .openingPhrase;

    }


    return (
        "Every step you take opens the door "
        + "to something new."
    );

}


/*
 * =========================================================
 * Companion Pick Message
 * =========================================================
 */


function getCompanionPickMessage(
    selected
) {

    if (
        selected.recommendation &&
        selected.recommendation.openingPhrase
    ) {

        return selected
            .recommendation
            .openingPhrase;

    }


    return (
        "I found something that might "
        + "spark your curiosity."
    );

}


/*
 * =========================================================
 * Companion Visual
 * =========================================================
 *
 * The character theme system may eventually provide an
 * avatar/image URL.
 *
 * We therefore support:
 *
 *     theme.avatar
 *     theme.character_image
 *     theme.characterImage
 *     selected.avatar
 *     selected.image
 *
 * without requiring them today.
 *
 * If none exists, the dashboard keeps a visual placeholder.
 *
 * =========================================================
 */


function updateCompanionVisual(
    selected
) {

    const visual =
        document.getElementById(
            "companion-visual"
        );

    const placeholder =
        document.getElementById(
            "companion-placeholder"
        );


    if (!visual) {

        return;

    }


    /*
     * =========================================================
     * CONFIGURABLE CHARACTER ARTWORK
     * =========================================================
     *
     * character-artwork.js owns the mapping between:
     *
     *     character ID
     *          ?
     *     approved front card
     *
     * app.js does not contain character-specific artwork paths.
     *
     * This allows artwork to be replaced or new characters
     * to be added without changing the dashboard logic.
     */


    let artwork = null;


    if (
        typeof getCharacterArtwork ===
        "function"
    ) {

        artwork =
            getCharacterArtwork(
                selected.id
            );

    }


    /*
     * =========================================================
     * FRONT CARD RESOLUTION
     * =========================================================
     */

    const imageSource =
        artwork &&
        (
            artwork.frontCard ||
            artwork.front_card ||
            artwork.frontCardUrl ||
            artwork.front_card_url ||
            artwork.src ||
            artwork.image
        );


    /*
     * =========================================================
     * CONFIGURED FRONT CARD
     * =========================================================
     */

    if (imageSource) {

        visual.style.backgroundImage =
            "url('" +
            imageSource +
            "')";


        visual.classList.add(
            "has-character-image"
        );


        visual.classList.add(
            "has-character-front-card"
        );


        if (placeholder) {

            placeholder.style.display =
                "none";

        }


        visual.dataset.characterArtwork =
            selected.id;


        return;

    }


    /*
     * =========================================================
     * LEGACY ARTWORK FALLBACK
     * =========================================================
     *
     * Keep compatibility with existing characters.js
     * artwork fields while the new artwork registry is
     * introduced.
     */

    const legacyImageSource =
        selected.avatar ||
        selected.image ||
        selected.avatarUrl ||
        null;


    if (legacyImageSource) {

        visual.style.backgroundImage =
            "url('" +
            legacyImageSource +
            "')";


        visual.classList.add(
            "has-character-image"
        );


        visual.classList.remove(
            "has-character-front-card"
        );


        if (placeholder) {

            placeholder.style.display =
                "none";

        }


        visual.dataset.characterArtwork =
            selected.id;


        return;

    }


    /*
     * =========================================================
     * NO ARTWORK
     * =========================================================
     *
     * Never invent artwork.
     *
     * If a character does not have a configured front card,
     * retain the existing character placeholder.
     */

    visual.style.backgroundImage =
        "";


    visual.classList.remove(
        "has-character-image"
    );


    visual.classList.remove(
        "has-character-front-card"
    );


    delete visual.dataset.characterArtwork;


    if (placeholder) {

        placeholder.style.display =
            "";


        placeholder.textContent =
            getCharacterSymbol(
                selected.id
            );

    }

}

function getCharacterSymbol(
    requestedCharacterId
) {

    const symbols = {

        yeti: "❄️",

        clara: "✦",

        luminate: "💡",

        alix: "🗺️",

        adri: "🌍",

        antoine: "📖",

        grinch: "☠️",

        julie: "🌿",

        lea: "✨",

        mathis: "⚡",

        naomi: "🎨",

        nora: "♟️",

        simon: "◇",

        zoe: "⚡",

        jeng: "🐻",

        layla: "🌙"

    };


    return (
        symbols[
            requestedCharacterId
        ] || "✨"
    );

}


/*
 * =========================================================
 * Character Theme Integration
 * =========================================================
 *
 * Backend endpoint:
 *
 *     GET /character-theme
 *
 * Inputs:
 *
 *     character_id
 *     world_id
 *     primary_language
 *     secondary_language
 *
 * The backend remains responsible for resolving the theme.
 *
 * =========================================================
 */


async function loadCharacterTheme() {

    if (!learnerContext) {

        console.error(
            "Cannot load theme without learner context."
        );

        return;

    }


    if (!characterId) {

        console.error(
            "Cannot load theme without character ID."
        );

        return;

    }


    const worldId =
        learnerContext.worldId;


    const primaryLanguage =
        learnerContext.primaryLanguage;


    const secondaryLanguage =
        learnerContext.secondaryLanguage;


    try {

        /*
         * -------------------------------------------------
         * Build query
         * -------------------------------------------------
         */

        const queryParameters = {

            character_id:
                characterId,

            world_id:
                worldId,

            primary_language:
                primaryLanguage

        };


        /*
         * Only send secondary language when
         * one actually exists.
         */

        if (secondaryLanguage) {

            queryParameters.secondary_language =
                secondaryLanguage;

        }


        const query =
            new URLSearchParams(
                queryParameters
            );


        /*
         * -------------------------------------------------
         * Request resolved theme
         * -------------------------------------------------
         */

        const response =
            await fetch(

                "http://127.0.0.1:8000/character-theme?" +
                query.toString()

            );


        if (!response.ok) {

            throw new Error(

                "Theme API request failed: " +
                response.status

            );

        }


        const themeResponse =
            await response.json();


        activeDashboardTheme =
            themeResponse;


        /*
         * -------------------------------------------------
         * Delegate presentation to theme.js
         * -------------------------------------------------
         */

        if (
            typeof applyCharacterTheme !==
            "function"
        ) {

            console.error(

                "applyCharacterTheme() was not found. " +
                "Make sure theme.js is loaded before app.js."

            );

            return;

        }


        applyCharacterTheme(
            themeResponse
        );


        /*
         * -------------------------------------------------
         * Apply optional theme-specific presentation
         * -------------------------------------------------
         */

        applyThemePresentation(
            themeResponse
        );


        console.log(
            "Character theme loaded:",
            characterId
        );


        console.log(
            "World:",
            worldId
        );


        console.log(
            "Primary language:",
            primaryLanguage
        );


        console.log(
            "Secondary language:",
            secondaryLanguage
        );


    } catch (error) {

        /*
         * Theme failure must NOT break the dashboard.
         */

        console.error(
            "Unable to load character theme:",
            error
        );


        /*
         * Keep the EasyChatBot core UI functional.
         */

    }

}


/*
 * =========================================================
 * Optional Theme Presentation
 * =========================================================
 *
 * theme.js owns CSS variable application.
 *
 * This function handles optional presentation metadata
 * without making the dashboard dependent on it.
 *
 * =========================================================
 */


function applyThemePresentation(
    themeResponse
) {

    if (!themeResponse) {

        return;

    }


    const theme =
        themeResponse.theme || {};


    const root =
        document.documentElement;


    /*
     * Character metadata
     */

    if (
        themeResponse.character_id
    ) {

        root.dataset.character =
            themeResponse.character_id;

    }


    /*
     * World metadata
     */

    if (
        themeResponse.world_id
    ) {

        root.dataset.world =
            themeResponse.world_id;

    }


    /*
     * Theme name
     */

    if (
        theme.name
    ) {

        root.dataset.theme =
            theme.name;

    }


    /*
     * Atmosphere
     */

    if (
        theme.atmosphere
    ) {

        if (
            typeof theme.atmosphere ===
            "string"
        ) {

            root.dataset.atmosphere =
                theme.atmosphere;

        }

        else if (
            typeof theme.atmosphere ===
            "object"
        ) {

            if (
                theme.atmosphere.id
            ) {

                root.dataset.atmosphere =
                    theme.atmosphere.id;

            }


            if (
                theme.atmosphere.type
            ) {

                root.dataset.atmosphereType =
                    theme.atmosphere.type;

            }

        }

    }

}


/*
 * =========================================================
 * Dashboard Navigation
 * =========================================================
 *
 * Home navigation is currently presentation-only.
 *
 * We do not introduce routing here yet.
 *
 * =========================================================
 */


function initialiseNavigation() {

    const navigationItems =
        document.querySelectorAll(
            ".dashboard-nav-item"
        );


    navigationItems.forEach(
        function (item) {

            item.addEventListener(
                "click",
                function (event) {

                    event.preventDefault();


                    navigationItems.forEach(
                        function (navItem) {

                            navItem.classList.remove(
                                "active"
                            );

                        }
                    );


                    item.classList.add(
                        "active"
                    );


                    const section =
                        item.dataset.section;


                    console.log(
                        "Dashboard navigation:",
                        section
                    );

                }
            );

        }
    );

}


/*
 * =========================================================
 * Dashboard Actions
 * =========================================================
 */


function initialiseDashboardActions() {

    /*
     * -----------------------------------------------------
     * Start Conversation
     * -----------------------------------------------------
     */

    const conversationButton =
        document.getElementById(
            "start-conversation-button"
        );


    if (conversationButton) {

        conversationButton.addEventListener(
            "click",
            function () {

                focusConversation();

            }
        );

    }


    /*
     * -----------------------------------------------------
     * Global dashboard buttons
     * -----------------------------------------------------
     */

    const primaryButtons =
        document.querySelectorAll(
            ".primary-dashboard-button"
        );


    primaryButtons.forEach(
        function (button) {

            if (
                button.id ===
                "start-conversation-button"
            ) {

                return;

            }


            button.addEventListener(
                "click",
                function () {

                    console.log(
                        "Dashboard action:",
                        button.textContent.trim()
                    );

                }
            );

        }
    );

}


/*
 * =========================================================
 * Conversation Focus
 * =========================================================
 *
 * The new Home dashboard intentionally does not embed the
 * old recommendation/chat demo.
 *
 * The real Chat experience remains the next application
 * destination.
 *
 * For the current single-page development frontend, we
 * provide a graceful fallback if a message field exists.
 *
 * =========================================================
 */


function focusConversation() {

    const message =
        document.getElementById(
            "message"
        );


    if (message) {

        message.focus();

        return;

    }


    console.log(
        "Conversation requested for:",
        character
    );


    /*
     * Future routing:
     *
     * window.location.href =
     *     "/chat";
     *
     * is deliberately NOT activated yet.
     */

}


/*
 * =========================================================
 * Chat
 * =========================================================
 *
 * Kept for compatibility with the existing backend chat
 * endpoint and future Chat UI.
 *
 * The important difference is that the character is now
 * derived from the learner's existing companion.
 *
 * =========================================================
 */


async function sendMessage() {

    const messageElement =
        document.getElementById(
            "message"
        );


    const researchElement =
        document.getElementById(
            "research"
        );


    const responseElement =
        document.getElementById(
            "response"
        );


    if (!messageElement) {

        console.error(
            "Chat message field not found."
        );

        return;

    }


    const message =
        messageElement.value.trim();


    const research =
        researchElement
            ? researchElement.checked
            : false;


    if (!message) {

        if (responseElement) {

            responseElement.textContent =
                "Please enter a message.";

        }

        return;

    }


    if (!character) {

        if (responseElement) {

            responseElement.textContent =
                "Your companion is not available.";

        }

        return;

    }


    if (responseElement) {

        responseElement.textContent =
            "Your companion is thinking...";

    }


    try {

        const response =
            await fetch(

                "http://127.0.0.1:8000/chat",

                {

                    method:
                        "POST",


                    headers: {

                        "Content-Type":
                            "application/json"

                    },


                    body:
                        JSON.stringify({

                            user_id:
                                "demo-user",


                            /*
                             * Preserve the existing backend
                             * contract.
                             *
                             * The backend currently receives
                             * the character name.
                             */

                            character:
                                character,


                            message:
                                message,


                            research:
                                research

                        })

                }

            );


        if (!response.ok) {

            throw new Error(

                "API request failed: " +
                response.status

            );

        }


        const data =
            await response.json();


        if (responseElement) {

            responseElement.textContent =
                JSON.stringify(

                    data,

                    null,

                    2

                );

        }


    } catch (error) {

        console.error(

            "EasyChatBot request failed:",

            error

        );


        if (responseElement) {

            responseElement.textContent =
                "Unable to connect to EasyChatBot. " +
                "Please make sure the backend is running.\n\n" +
                error.message;

        }

    }

}


/*
 * =========================================================
 * Dashboard Error State
 * =========================================================
 */


function showDashboardError(
    message
) {

    console.error(
        "EasyChatBot dashboard error:",
        message
    );


    const title =
        document.getElementById(
            "companion-title"
        );


    const description =
        document.getElementById(
            "companion-message"
        );


    if (title) {

        title.textContent =
            "Your companion is unavailable";

    }


    if (description) {

        description.textContent =
            message;

    }

}


/*
 * =========================================================
 * DOM Utility
 * =========================================================
 */


function setText(
    elementId,
    value
) {

    const element =
        document.getElementById(
            elementId
        );


    if (!element) {

        return;

    }


    element.textContent =
        value;

}

    return activeDashboardTheme;

}

    return character;

}

 * =========================================================
 * Application Startup
 * =========================================================
 *
 * We deliberately do NOT:
 *
 *     selectCharacter("yeti")
 *
 * We deliberately do NOT:
 *
 *     render character buttons
 *
 * We deliberately do NOT:
 *
 *     ask the learner to choose a character.
 *
 * The learner's existing companion is loaded from the
 * learner context.
 *
 * =========================================================
 */


window.addEventListener(
    "DOMContentLoaded",
    function () {

        initialiseDashboard();

    }
);

"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  getFunctionalityHealth,
  type FunctionalityHealth,
  type FunctionalityHealthPayload,
  type OperationsEnvironment,
} from "@/app/lib/api/functionalityHealth";
import {
  resumeFunctionality,
  stopFunctionality,
} from "@/app/lib/api/functionalityHealthControls";
import styles from "./FunctionalityHealthDashboard.module.css";

const statusText: Record<FunctionalityHealth["status"], string> = {
  healthy: "Healthy",
  degraded: "Degraded",
  unavailable: "Unavailable",
  maintenance: "Maintenance",
  disabled: "Disabled",
  unknown: "Unknown",
};

const statusClass: Record<FunctionalityHealth["status"], string> = {
  healthy: styles.statusHealthy,
  degraded: styles.statusDegraded,
  unavailable: styles.statusIncident,
  maintenance: styles.statusMaintenance,
  disabled: styles.statusDisabled,
  unknown: styles.statusUnknown,
};

function Icon({
  type,
}: {
  type:
    | "health"
    | "check"
    | "warning"
    | "incident"
    | "maintenance"
    | "search"
    | "refresh"
    | "download"
    | "close";
}) {
  const common = {
    width: 18,
    height: 18,
    viewBox: "0 0 24 24",
    fill: "none",
    stroke: "currentColor",
    strokeWidth: 2,
    strokeLinecap: "round" as const,
    strokeLinejoin: "round" as const,
    "aria-hidden": true,
  };

  if (type === "check") {
    return (
      <svg {...common}>
        <path d="m5 12 4 4L19 6" />
      </svg>
    );
  }

  if (type === "warning") {
    return (
      <svg {...common}>
        <path d="M10.3 3.6 2.2 18a2 2 0 0 0 1.7 3h16.2a2 2 0 0 0 1.7-3L13.7 3.6a2 2 0 0 0-3.4 0Z" />
        <path d="M12 9v4" />
        <path d="M12 17h.01" />
      </svg>
    );
  }

  if (type === "incident") {
    return (
      <svg {...common}>
        <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9" />
        <path d="M12 8v5" />
        <path d="M12 16h.01" />
      </svg>
    );
  }

  if (type === "maintenance") {
    return (
      <svg {...common}>
        <path d="m14.7 6.3 3 3" />
        <path d="m3 21 7.8-7.8" />
        <path d="M14 4a5 5 0 0 0 6 6l-4 4-6-6 4-4Z" />
      </svg>
    );
  }

  if (type === "search") {
    return (
      <svg {...common}>
        <circle cx="11" cy="11" r="7" />
        <path d="m20 20-4-4" />
      </svg>
    );
  }

  if (type === "refresh") {
    return (
      <svg {...common}>
        <path d="M20 11a8.1 8.1 0 0 0-14.8-4L3 10" />
        <path d="M3 5v5h5" />
        <path d="M4 13a8.1 8.1 0 0 0 14.8 4L21 14" />
        <path d="M21 19v-5h-5" />
      </svg>
    );
  }

  if (type === "download") {
    return (
      <svg {...common}>
        <path d="M12 3v12" />
        <path d="m7 10 5 5 5-5" />
        <path d="M5 21h14" />
      </svg>
    );
  }

  if (type === "close") {
    return (
      <svg {...common}>
        <path d="m6 6 12 12" />
        <path d="m18 6-12 12" />
      </svg>
    );
  }

  return (
    <svg {...common}>
      <path d="M4 17V9" />
      <path d="M9 17V5" />
      <path d="M14 17v-7" />
      <path d="M19 17V3" />
    </svg>
  );
}

function StatusBadge({
  status,
}: {
  status: FunctionalityHealth["status"];
}) {
  const icon =
    status === "healthy"
      ? "check"
      : status === "degraded"
        ? "warning"
        : status === "unavailable"
          ? "incident"
          : status === "maintenance"
            ? "maintenance"
            : "incident";

  return (
    <span className={`${styles.statusBadge} ${statusClass[status]}`}>
      <Icon type={icon} />
      {statusText[status]}
    </span>
  );
}

function HealthRing({ score }: { score: number | null }) {
  const safeScore = score == null ? 0 : Math.max(0, Math.min(100, score));
  const circumference = 2 * Math.PI * 42;
  const offset = circumference - (safeScore / 100) * circumference;

  return (
    <div className={styles.healthRing}>
      <svg viewBox="0 0 100 100" className={styles.healthRingSvg}>
        <circle
          cx="50"
          cy="50"
          r="42"
          className={styles.healthRingTrack}
        />
        <circle
          cx="50"
          cy="50"
          r="42"
          className={styles.healthRingValue}
          strokeDasharray={circumference}
          strokeDashoffset={offset}
        />
      </svg>
      <div className={styles.healthRingLabel}>
        <strong>{score == null ? "�" : `${score}%`}</strong>
        <span>Health</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function SummaryCard({
  label,
  value,
  icon,
  tone,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: string | number;
  icon: "check" | "warning" | "incident" | "maintenance";
  tone: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className={styles.summaryCard}>
      <div className={`${styles.summaryIcon} ${tone}`}>
        <Icon type={icon} />
      </div>
      <div>
        <div className={styles.summaryValue}>{value}</div>
        <div className={styles.summaryLabel}>{label}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function CategoryChart({
  functionalities,
}: {
  functionalities: FunctionalityHealth[];
}) {
  const categories = useMemo(() => {
    const grouped = new Map<string, number>();

    functionalities.forEach((item) => {
      grouped.set(
        item.category,
        (grouped.get(item.category) ?? 0) + 1,
      );
    });

    return Array.from(grouped.entries())
      .sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])
      .slice(0, 6);
  }, [functionalities]);

  const max = Math.max(...categories.map(([, count]) => count), 1);

  return (
    <div className={styles.chartCard}>
      <div className={styles.cardHeader}>
        <div>
          <h2>Status by Category</h2>
          <p>Current functionality distribution</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className={styles.barChart}>
        {categories.map(([category, count]) => (
          <div className={styles.barColumn} key={category}>
            <div className={styles.barValue}>{count}</div>
            <div className={styles.barTrack}>
              <div
                className={styles.barFill}
                style={{
                  height: `${Math.max(12, (count / max) * 100)}%`,
                }}
              />
            </div>
            <div className={styles.barLabel}>{category}</div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function StatusDistribution({
  counts,
  functionalities,
}: {
  counts: FunctionalityHealthPayload["counts"];
  functionalities: FunctionalityHealth[];
}) {
  const total = functionalities.length;

  const healthy = counts.healthy ?? 0;
  const degraded = counts.degraded ?? 0;
  const incident = counts.incident ?? 0;

  const healthyPct = total ? (healthy / total) * 100 : 0;
  const degradedPct = total ? (degraded / total) * 100 : 0;
  const incidentPct = total ? (incident / total) * 100 : 0;

  const gradient = `conic-gradient(
    #10b981 0 ${healthyPct}%,
    #f59e0b ${healthyPct}% ${healthyPct + degradedPct}%,
    #ef4444 ${healthyPct + degradedPct}% ${healthyPct + degradedPct + incidentPct}%,
    #e2e8f0 ${healthyPct + degradedPct + incidentPct}% 100%
  )`;

  return (
    <div className={styles.chartCard}>
      <div className={styles.cardHeader}>
        <div>
          <h2>Functionality Status</h2>
          <p>Live operational distribution</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className={styles.statusDistribution}>
        <div
          className={styles.donut}
          style={{ background: gradient }}
          aria-label={`${total} total functionalities`}
        >
          <div className={styles.donutInner}>
            <strong>{total}</strong>
            <span>Total</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className={styles.legend}>
          <div>
            <span className={`${styles.legendDot} ${styles.dotHealthy}`} />
            <span>Healthy</span>
            <strong>{healthy}</strong>
          </div>
          <div>
            <span className={`${styles.legendDot} ${styles.dotDegraded}`} />
            <span>Degraded</span>
            <strong>{degraded}</strong>
          </div>
          <div>
            <span className={`${styles.legendDot} ${styles.dotIncident}`} />
            <span>Incident</span>
            <strong>{incident}</strong>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function FunctionalityTable({
  functionalities,
  onSelect,
  selectedId,
}: {
  functionalities: FunctionalityHealth[];
  onSelect: (id: string) => void;
  selectedId: string | null;
}) {
  return (
    <div className={styles.tableCard}>
      <div className={styles.cardHeader}>
        <div>
          <h2>Functionality Overview</h2>
          <p>
            All EasyChatBot capabilities and their current operational
            status.
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          className={styles.secondaryButton}
          onClick={() => {
            const headers = [
              "Functionality",
              "Category",
              "Status",
              "Health",
              "Latency",
              "Provider",
              "Risk",
            ];

            const rows = functionalities.map((item) => [
              item.name,
              item.category,
              statusText[item.status],
              item.health_score == null ? "" : `${item.health_score}%`,
              item.latency_ms == null ? "" : `${item.latency_ms} ms`,
              item.provider ?? "",
              item.risk_level,
            ]);

            const csv = [headers, ...rows]
              .map((row) =>
                row
                  .map((value) => `"${String(value).replaceAll('"', '""')}"`)
                  .join(","),
              )
              .join("\n");

            const blob = new Blob([csv], {
              type: "text/csv;charset=utf-8;",
            });

            const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
            const anchor = document.createElement("a");
            anchor.href = url;
            anchor.download = "easychatbot-functionality-health.csv";
            anchor.click();
            URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
          }}
        >
          <Icon type="download" />
          Export
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className={styles.tableFilters}>
        <div className={styles.searchBox}>
          <Icon type="search" />
          <input
            aria-label="Search functionalities"
            placeholder="Search functionalities..."
            onChange={() => undefined}
          />
        </div>

        <button type="button" className={styles.filterButton}>
          All categories
          <span>?</span>
        </button>

        <button type="button" className={styles.filterButton}>
          All statuses
          <span>?</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className={styles.tableWrapper}>
        <table className={styles.healthTable}>
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th>Functionality</th>
              <th>Category</th>
              <th>Status</th>
              <th>Health</th>
              <th>Latency</th>
              <th>Provider</th>
              <th>Last Checked</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {functionalities.map((item) => (
              <tr
                key={item.functionality_id}
                className={
                  selectedId === item.functionality_id
                    ? styles.selectedRow
                    : ""
                }
                onClick={() => onSelect(item.functionality_id)}
              >
                <td>
                  <div className={styles.functionalityName}>
                    <span className={styles.functionalityIcon}>?</span>
                    <div>
                      <strong>{item.name}</strong>
                      <span>{item.capability ?? item.description}</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </td>
                <td>{item.category}</td>
                <td>
                  <StatusBadge status={item.status} />
                </td>
                <td>
                  <strong
                    className={
                      item.status === "unavailable"
                        ? styles.valueIncident
                        : item.status === "degraded"
                          ? styles.valueDegraded
                          : styles.valueNormal
                    }
                  >
                    {item.health_score == null
                      ? "�"
                      : `${item.health_score}%`}
                  </strong>
                </td>
                <td>
                  <span
                    className={
                      item.latency_ms != null && item.latency_ms > 1000
                        ? styles.latencyWarning
                        : ""
                    }
                  >
                    {item.latency_ms == null
                      ? "�"
                      : `${item.latency_ms} ms`}
                  </span>
                </td>
                <td>{item.provider ?? "�"}</td>
                <td>
                  {item.checked_at
                    ? new Date(item.checked_at).toLocaleString()
                    : "�"}
                </td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>

      <div className={styles.tableFooter}>
        Showing {functionalities.length} functionality
        {functionalities.length === 1 ? "" : "ies"}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function FunctionalityHealthDashboard() {
  const [data, setData] = useState<FunctionalityHealthPayload | null>(null);
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [environment, setEnvironment] = useState<OperationsEnvironment>("adults");
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [controlBusy, setControlBusy] = useState(false);
  const [controlError, setControlError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [stopConfirmationOpen, setStopConfirmationOpen] = useState(false);
  const [stopReason, setStopReason] = useState("");

  const refresh = useCallback(async () => {
    setRefreshing(true);

    try {
      const next = await getFunctionalityHealth(environment);

      setData(next);
      setError(null);

      setSelectedId((current) =>
        current &&
        next.functionalities.some(
          (item) => item.functionality_id === current,
        )
          ? current
          : next.functionalities[0]?.functionality_id ?? null,
      );
    } catch (cause) {
      setError(
        cause instanceof Error
          ? cause.message
          : "Unable to load live health.",
      );
    } finally {
      setRefreshing(false);
    }
  }, [environment]);

  useEffect(() => {
    void refresh();

    const timer = window.setInterval(() => {
      void refresh();
    }, 15_000);

    return () => window.clearInterval(timer);
  }, [refresh]);

  const selected = useMemo(
    () =>
      data?.functionalities.find(
        (item) => item.functionality_id === selectedId,
      ) ?? null,
    [data, selectedId],
  );

  const selectedAvailability = selected?.availability ?? "enabled";
  const selectedIsDisabled = selectedAvailability === "disabled";

  const executeStop = async () => {
    if (!selected || !stopReason.trim()) {
      return;
    }

    setControlBusy(true);
    setControlError(null);

    try {
      await stopFunctionality(
        selected.functionality_id,
        environment,
        stopReason.trim(),
      );

      setStopConfirmationOpen(false);
      setStopReason("");
      await refresh();
    } catch (cause) {
      setControlError(
        cause instanceof Error
          ? cause.message
          : "Unable to stop this functionality.",
      );
    } finally {
      setControlBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const executeResume = async () => {
    if (!selected) {
      return;
    }

    setControlBusy(true);
    setControlError(null);

    try {
      await resumeFunctionality(
        selected.functionality_id,
        environment,
      );

      await refresh();
    } catch (cause) {
      setControlError(
        cause instanceof Error
          ? cause.message
          : "Unable to resume this functionality.",
      );
    } finally {
      setControlBusy(false);
    }
  };

  if (!data) {
    return (
      <main className={styles.loadingPage}>
        <div className={styles.loadingCard}>
          {error ? (
            <div role="alert" className={styles.error}>
              {error}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className={styles.loadingText} aria-live="polite">
              Loading Functionality Health...
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </main>
    );
  }

  const counts = data.counts;

  return (
    <main className={styles.page}>
      <div className={styles.content}>
        <header className={styles.pageHeader}>
          <div>
            <div className={styles.breadcrumb}>
              Operations Center <span>�</span>{" "}
              <strong>Functionality Health</strong>
            </div>

            <h1>Functionality Health</h1>

            <p>
              Real-time operational health across all EasyChatBot
              capabilities.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className={styles.headerActions}>
            <div role="group" aria-label="Operations Center environment" className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border border-white/10 bg-white/5 p-1">
              {(["kids", "adults"] as const).map((option) => (
                <button key={option} type="button" aria-pressed={environment === option} onClick={() => setEnvironment(option)} className={`rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-sm font-semibold transition ${environment === option ? "bg-white text-slate-900" : "text-slate-300 hover:bg-white/10"}`}>
                  {option === "kids" ? "Kids" : "Adults"}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
            <div className={styles.updated}>
              <span>Last updated</span>
              <strong>
                {new Date(data.generated_at).toLocaleString()}
              </strong>
            </div>

            <button
              type="button"
              className={styles.primaryButton}
              onClick={() => void refresh()}
              disabled={refreshing}
            >
              <Icon type="refresh" />
              {refreshing ? "Refreshing..." : "Refresh"}
            </button>
          </div>
        </header>

        <section className={styles.summaryGrid}>
          <div className={styles.platformHealthCard}>
            <HealthRing score={data.platform_health_score} />
            <div>
              <span>Platform Health</span>
              <strong>Overall platform status</strong>
              <small>User impact: {data.user_impact}</small>
            </div>
          </div>

          <SummaryCard
            label="Healthy"
            value={counts.healthy ?? 0}
            icon="check"
            tone={styles.iconGreen}
          />

          <SummaryCard
            label="Degraded"
            value={counts.degraded ?? 0}
            icon="warning"
            tone={styles.iconAmber}
          />

          <SummaryCard
            label="Incident"
            value={counts.incident ?? 0}
            icon="incident"
            tone={styles.iconRed}
          />

          <SummaryCard
            label="Maintenance"
            value={counts.maintenance ?? 0}
            icon="maintenance"
            tone={styles.iconBlue}
          />
        </section>

        {data.attention.length > 0 && (
          <section className={styles.attentionBanner}>
            <div className={styles.attentionIcon}>
              <Icon type="warning" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <strong>
                {data.attention.length} functionality
                {data.attention.length === 1 ? "" : "ies"} require attention
              </strong>
              <span>
                Review the exception before taking operational action.
              </span>
            </div>
          </section>
        )}

        <section className={styles.chartGrid}>
          <StatusDistribution
            counts={counts}
            functionalities={data.functionalities}
          />
          <CategoryChart functionalities={data.functionalities} />
        </section>

        <section className={styles.workspace}>
          <FunctionalityTable
            functionalities={data.functionalities}
            selectedId={selectedId}
            onSelect={setSelectedId}
          />

          {selected && (
            <aside className={styles.detailsPanel}>
              <div className={styles.detailsHeader}>
                <div>
                  <span>Functionality Details</span>
                  <h2>{selected.name}</h2>
                </div>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  aria-label="Close functionality details"
                  className={styles.iconButton}
                  onClick={() => setSelectedId(null)}
                >
                  <Icon type="close" />
                </button>
              </div>

              <StatusBadge status={selected.status} />

              <p className={styles.detailsDescription}>
                {selected.description}
              </p>

              <div className={styles.detailsGrid}>
                <div>
                  <span>Functionality ID</span>
                  <strong>{selected.functionality_id}</strong>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span>Category</span>
                  <strong>{selected.category}</strong>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span>Health Score</span>
                  <strong>{selected.health_score ?? "�"}%</strong>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span>Latency</span>
                  <strong>{selected.latency_ms ?? "�"} ms</strong>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span>Provider</span>
                  <strong>{selected.provider ?? "�"}</strong>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span>Model</span>
                  <strong>{selected.model ?? "�"}</strong>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span>Risk Level</span>
                  <strong>{selected.risk_level}</strong>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span>Last Checked</span>
                  <strong>
                    {selected.checked_at
                      ? new Date(selected.checked_at).toLocaleString()
                      : "�"}
                  </strong>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className={styles.assessment}>
                <span>Current assessment</span>
                <p>{selected.reason}</p>
              </div>

              <div className={styles.controlNotice}>
                <strong>Operational controls</strong>

                {selectedIsDisabled ? (
                  <>
                    <span>
                      This functionality is currently stopped for the{" "}
                      {environment === "kids" ? "Kids" : "Adults"} environment.
                    </span>

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className={styles.resumeButton}
                      onClick={() => void executeResume()}
                      disabled={controlBusy}
                    >
                      {controlBusy ? "Resuming..." : "Resume functionality"}
                    </button>
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <span>
                      Stop is a backend-authorised operational action and is
                      recorded with an audit reason.
                    </span>

                    {!stopConfirmationOpen ? (
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        className={styles.stopButton}
                        onClick={() => {
                          setControlError(null);
                          setStopConfirmationOpen(true);
                        }}
                        disabled={controlBusy}
                      >
                        Stop functionality
                      </button>
                    ) : (
                      <div className={styles.stopConfirmation}>
                        <strong>Confirm stop</strong>

                        <label htmlFor="functionality-stop-reason">
                          Reason <span aria-hidden="true">*</span>
                        </label>

                        <textarea
                          id="functionality-stop-reason"
                          value={stopReason}
                          onChange={(event) =>
                            setStopReason(event.target.value)
                          }
                          placeholder="Enter the reason for stopping this functionality..."
                          maxLength={2000}
                          disabled={controlBusy}
                          rows={4}
                        />

                        <div className={styles.controlActions}>
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            className={styles.cancelButton}
                            onClick={() => {
                              setStopConfirmationOpen(false);
                              setStopReason("");
                              setControlError(null);
                            }}
                            disabled={controlBusy}
                          >
                            Cancel
                          </button>

                          <button
                            type="button"
                            className={styles.confirmStopButton}
                            onClick={() => void executeStop()}
                            disabled={controlBusy || !stopReason.trim()}
                          >
                            {controlBusy ? "Stopping..." : "Confirm Stop"}
                          </button>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </>
                )}

                {controlError && (
                  <div role="alert" className={styles.controlError}>
                    {controlError}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </aside>
          )}
        </section>

        <footer className={styles.footer}>
          <span>EasyChatBot Operations Center</span>
          <span>Backend-authorised operational controls</span>
        </footer>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

const primaryItems: readonly NavItemDefinition[] = [
  ["Overview", "/operations-center", "overview"],
  ["Providers", "/operations-center/providers", "providers"],
  ["Models", "/operations-center/models", "models"],
  ["Usage & Costs", "/operations-center/usage", "usage"],
