function NavSection({ label, children }: { label: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <section className={styles.section}>
      <h2>{label}</h2>
      <div className={styles.items}>{children}</div>
    </section>
  );
}

function NavItem({
  label,
  href,
  icon,
  active,
}: {
  label: string;
  href: string;
  icon: string;
  active: boolean;
}) {
  return (
    <a
      href={href}
      aria-current={active ? "page" : undefined}
      className={`${styles.item} ${active ? styles.itemActive : ""}`}
    >
      <span className={styles.icon} aria-hidden="true">{icon}</span>
      <span>{label}</span>
    </a>
  );
}

function DisabledItem({ label }: { label: string }) {
  return (
    <div aria-disabled="true" className={`${styles.item} ${styles.itemDisabled}`}>
      <span className={styles.icon} aria-hidden="true">·</span>
      <span>{label}</span>
      <small>Soon</small>
    </div>
  );
}

export type ProjectStatus =
  | "on_track"
  | "at_risk"
  | "blocked"
  | "complete"
  | "unknown";

export type WorkstreamStatus =
  | "healthy"
  | "warning"
  | "blocked"
  | "complete"
  | "unknown";

export type SourceStatus =
  | "available"
  | "stale"
  | "unavailable";

export type Severity =
  | "low"
  | "medium"
  | "high"
  | "critical";

export type IssueStatus =
  | "open"
  | "in_progress"
  | "resolved"
  | "closed";

export type ActionStatus =
  | "open"
  | "in_progress"
  | "done"
  | "cancelled";

export interface WorkstreamSnapshot {
  workstream_id: string;
  name: string;
  completion_percent: number;
  status: WorkstreamStatus;
  source: string;
  updated_at: string | null;
}

export interface MetricSnapshot {
  key: string;
  label: string;
  value: string | number | boolean | null;
  unit: string | null;
  source: string;
  observed_at: string | null;
}

export interface IssueSnapshot {
  issue_id: string;
  project_id: string;
  title: string;
  severity: Severity;
  status: IssueStatus;
  workstream_id: string | null;
  owner: string | null;
  source: string;
}

export interface RiskSnapshot {
  risk_id: string;
  project_id: string;
  title: string;
  severity: Severity;
  status: string;
  workstream_id: string | null;
  owner: string | null;
  source: string;
}

export interface ActionSnapshot {
  action_id: string;
  project_id: string;
  title: string;
  priority: string;
  status: ActionStatus;
  owner: string | null;
  workstream_id: string | null;
  due_at: string | null;
  source: string;
}

export interface MilestoneSnapshot {
  milestone_id: string;
  project_id: string;
  name: string;
  status: string;
  target_date: string | null;
  source: string;
}

export interface SourceSnapshot {
  source_id: string;
  name: string;
  status: SourceStatus;
  updated_at: string | null;
  error: string | null;
}

export interface ProjectControlSnapshot {
  project_id: string;
  project_name: string;
  project_status: ProjectStatus;
  overall_progress_percent: number;
  current_milestone: string | null;
  current_workstream: string | null;
  release_readiness: string;
  open_blockers: number;
  open_risks: number;
  workstreams: WorkstreamSnapshot[];
  metrics: MetricSnapshot[];
  issues: IssueSnapshot[];
  risks: RiskSnapshot[];
  actions: ActionSnapshot[];
  milestones: MilestoneSnapshot[];
  sources: SourceSnapshot[];
  generated_at: string;
}

export class ProjectControlApiError extends Error {
  readonly status: number;

  constructor(
    message: string,
    status: number,
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "ProjectControlApiError";
    this.status = status;
  }
}

export async function getProjectControlSnapshot(
  projectId: string,
): Promise<ProjectControlSnapshot> {
  const encodedProjectId = encodeURIComponent(
    projectId,
  );

  const response = await fetch(
    `/project-control/projects/${encodedProjectId}`,
    {
      method: "GET",
      credentials: "include",
      headers: {
        Accept: "application/json",
      },
    },
  );

  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail = `Project Control API returned ${response.status}.`;

    try {
      const body = await response.json();

      if (
        body &&
        typeof body.detail === "string"
      ) {
        detail = body.detail;
      }
    } catch {
      // Preserve the safe generic API error.
    }

    throw new ProjectControlApiError(
      detail,
      response.status,
    );
  }

  return response.json() as Promise<ProjectControlSnapshot>;
}

import {
  useState,
  type FormEvent,
} from "react";

import {
  useNavigate,
  useSearchParams,
} from "react-router-dom";

import { useAuth } from "../../src/auth/useAuth";
import { AuthenticationApiError } from "../../src/auth/authApi";

export default function LoginPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();

  const { login } = useAuth();

  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");

  const [error, setError] =
    useState<string | null>(null);

  const [loading, setLoading] =
    useState(false);

  async function handleSubmit(
    event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>,
  ) {
    event.preventDefault();

    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);

    try {
      await login({
        email: email.trim(),
        password,
      });

      const requestedRedirect =
        searchParams.get("redirect");

      let redirect = "/project-control";

      if (requestedRedirect) {
        try {
          const decoded =
            decodeURIComponent(requestedRedirect);

          /*
           * Only permit internal application paths.
           *
           * This prevents the login redirect from becoming
           * an open redirect.
           */
          if (
            decoded.startsWith("/") &&
            !decoded.startsWith("//")
          ) {
            redirect = decoded;
          }
        } catch {
          /*
           * Keep the safe default.
           */
        }
      }

      navigate(
        redirect,
        {
          replace: true,
        },
      );
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof AuthenticationApiError
          ? err.message
          : "Unable to sign in.",
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <main
      style={{
        minHeight: "100vh",
        display: "grid",
        placeItems: "center",
        padding: "2rem",
      }}
    >
      <section
        aria-labelledby="login-title"
        style={{
          width: "100%",
          maxWidth: "420px",
        }}
      >
        <h1 id="login-title">
          EasyChatBot
        </h1>

        <p>
          Authorized access required.
        </p>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
          <label>
            Email

            <input
              type="email"
              autoComplete="username"
              value={email}
              onChange={(event) =>
                setEmail(event.target.value)
              }
              required
            />
          </label>

          <label>
            Password

            <input
              type="password"
              autoComplete="current-password"
              value={password}
              onChange={(event) =>
                setPassword(event.target.value)
              }
              required
            />
          </label>

          {error && (
            <p role="alert">
              {error}
            </p>
          )}

          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={loading}
          >
            {loading
              ? "Signing in�"
              : "Sign in"}
          </button>
        </form>
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\frontend\app\project-control\page.tsx

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import type { CSSProperties } from "react";

import {
  getProjectControlSnapshot,
  ProjectControlApiError,
  type ProjectControlSnapshot,
} from "@/app/lib/api/projectControl";

import styles from "./ProjectControlPage.module.css";

const DEFAULT_PROJECT_ID = "easychatbot";

// Deduplicate concurrent reads. This is deliberately limited to in-flight
// requests: a completed snapshot is never cached, so Refresh always asks the
// authoritative API for fresh data. It also prevents React StrictMode's
// development-only effect replay from issuing two HTTP GETs.
const inFlightSnapshots = new Map<
  string,
  Promise<ProjectControlSnapshot>
>();

function getSnapshotOnce(projectId: string): Promise<ProjectControlSnapshot> {
  const existing = inFlightSnapshots.get(projectId);
  if (existing) return existing;

  const request = getProjectControlSnapshot(projectId);
  inFlightSnapshots.set(projectId, request);

  const clearInFlight = () => {
    if (inFlightSnapshots.get(projectId) === request) {
      inFlightSnapshots.delete(projectId);
    }
  };

  // Handle both outcomes without creating an unhandled rejected promise.
  void request.then(clearInFlight, clearInFlight);

  return request;
}

function statusLabel(
  status: ProjectControlSnapshot["project_status"],
): string {
  switch (status) {
    case "on_track":
      return "ON TRACK";
    case "at_risk":
      return "AT RISK";
    case "blocked":
      return "BLOCKED";
    case "complete":
      return "COMPLETE";
    default:
      return "UNKNOWN";
  }
}

function statusTone(status: string): string {
  if (status === "on_track" || status === "available" || status === "complete" || status === "resolved" || status === "done") {
    return "success";
  }
  if (status === "at_risk" || status === "stale" || status === "in_progress") {
    return "warning";
  }
  if (status === "blocked" || status === "unavailable" || status === "open" || status === "cancelled") {
    return "danger";
  }
  return "neutral";
}

function severityTone(severity: string): string {
  const value = severity.toLowerCase();
  if (value === "critical" || value === "high") return "danger";
  if (value === "medium") return "warning";
  return "neutral";
}

function formatDate(value: string | null): string {
  if (!value) return "Not available";

  const date = new Date(value);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return value;

  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined, {
    dateStyle: "medium",
    timeStyle: "short",
  }).format(date);
}

function formatPercent(value: number): string {
  return `${Math.max(0, Math.min(100, value)).toFixed(1)}%`;
}

function formatWholePercent(value: number): string {
  return `${Math.max(0, Math.min(100, value)).toFixed(0)}%`;
}

function formatReadiness(value: string): string {
  const trimmed = value.trim();
  if (!trimmed) return "Not available";

  const numeric = Number(trimmed.replace(/%$/, ""));
  if (Number.isFinite(numeric)) {
    return `${Math.max(0, Math.min(100, numeric)).toFixed(1)}%`;
  }

  return trimmed;
}

function MetricValue({
  value,
  unit,
}: {
  value: string | number | boolean | null;
  unit: string | null;
}) {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return <>—</>;

  return (
    <>
      {String(value)}
      {unit ? <span className={styles.metricUnit}> {unit}</span> : null}
    </>
  );
}

export default function ProjectControlPage() {
  const [snapshot, setSnapshot] = useState<ProjectControlSnapshot | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // React StrictMode intentionally replays effects in development.
  // The initial authoritative GET must remain idempotent per mounted
  // component instance, while explicit Retry/Refresh actions remain
  // allowed to issue a fresh request.
  const initialLoadStartedRef = useRef(false);

  const loadSnapshot = useCallback(async (isRefresh = false) => {
    if (isRefresh) {
      setRefreshing(true);
    } else {
      setLoading(true);
    }

    setError(null);

    try {
      const result = await getSnapshotOnce(DEFAULT_PROJECT_ID);
      setSnapshot(result);
    } catch (cause) {
      if (cause instanceof ProjectControlApiError) {
        setError(cause.message);
      } else {
        setError("Unable to load Project Control data.");
      }
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
      setRefreshing(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (initialLoadStartedRef.current) {
      return;
    }

    initialLoadStartedRef.current = true;
    void loadSnapshot();
  }, [loadSnapshot]);

  const unavailableSources = useMemo(
    () =>
      snapshot?.sources.filter((source) => source.status !== "available") ?? [],
    [snapshot],
  );

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <main className={styles.page} aria-label="Project Control Center">
        <div className={styles.loadingPanel} role="status">
          <div className={styles.loaderOrb} aria-hidden="true">
            ✦
          </div>
          <p className={styles.eyebrow}>PROJECT CONTROL CENTER</p>
          <h1>Consulting the Observatory</h1>
          <p>Loading project control data…</p>
        </div>
      </main>
    );
  }

  if (error) {
    return (
      <main className={styles.page} aria-label="Project Control Center">
        <section className={styles.errorPanel} role="alert" aria-label="Project Control error">
          <p className={styles.eyebrow}>PROJECT CONTROL CENTER</p>
          <h1>Project intelligence unavailable</h1>
          <p>{error}</p>
          <button
            type="button"
            className={styles.primaryButton}
            onClick={() => void loadSnapshot()}
          >
            <span aria-hidden="true">↻</span>
            Retry
          </button>
        </section>
      </main>
    );
  }

  if (!snapshot) {
    return (
      <main className={styles.page} aria-label="Project Control Center">
        <section className={styles.emptyPanel}>
          <p className={styles.eyebrow}>PROJECT CONTROL CENTER</p>
          <h1>No project snapshot</h1>
          <p>No authoritative project-control snapshot is available.</p>
        </section>
      </main>
    );
  }

  const progress = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, snapshot.overall_progress_percent));

  return (
    <main className={styles.page} aria-label="Project Control Center">
      <div className={styles.atmosphere} aria-hidden="true" />

      <div className={styles.content}>
        <nav className={styles.breadcrumb} aria-label="Breadcrumb">
          <span>Home</span>
          <span aria-hidden="true">›</span>
          <span>Operations Center</span>
          <span aria-hidden="true">›</span>
          <strong>Project Control Center</strong>
        </nav>

        <section className={styles.hero}>
          <div className={styles.heroImage} role="img" aria-label="EasyChatBot Academy observatory library" />
          <div className={styles.heroOverlay} />

          <div className={styles.heroContent}>
            <p className={styles.eyebrow}>PROJECT CONTROL CENTER</p>
            <h1>
              From Plan <span>to Impact</span>
            </h1>
            <p className={styles.heroSubtitle}>
              Real-time project intelligence from authoritative sources.
            </p>

            <div className={styles.principles} aria-label="Project control principles">
              <div>
                <span className={styles.principleIcon}>◇</span>
                <span>Clear<br />Progress</span>
              </div>
              <div>
                <span className={styles.principleIcon}>▥</span>
                <span>Informed<br />Decisions</span>
              </div>
              <div>
                <span className={styles.principleIcon}>♧</span>
                <span>Aligned<br />Teams</span>
              </div>
              <div>
                <span className={styles.principleIcon}>✦</span>
                <span>A Brighter<br />Tomorrow</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <blockquote className={styles.heroQuote}>
            “Disciplined execution today,<br />
            a brighter tomorrow.”
          </blockquote>
        </section>

        <section className={styles.projectBar} aria-label="Project identity">
          <div className={styles.projectIdentity}>
            <div className={styles.projectEmblem} aria-hidden="true">✦</div>
            <div>
              <div className={styles.projectNameRow}>
                <h2>{snapshot.project_name}</h2>
                <span
                  className={styles.statusBadge}
                  data-tone={statusTone(snapshot.project_status)}
                >
                  ● {statusLabel(snapshot.project_status)}
                </span>
              </div>
              <p>AI-powered language learning for a brighter tomorrow.</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className={styles.projectControls}>
            <div className={styles.updated}>
              <span>Last updated</span>
              <strong>{formatDate(snapshot.generated_at)}</strong>
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"

          )}

          {unavailableSources.length > 0 && (

import type { GovernanceTimelineEvent } from "../../services/governanceTimelineApi";

type Props = {
  events: GovernanceTimelineEvent[];
};

export default function GovernanceEvidenceExplorer({ events }: Props) {
  return (
    <section className="governance-section-card">
      <div className="governance-section-card__heading">
        <div>
          <span className="section-eyebrow">GOVERNANCE EVIDENCE</span>
          <h2>Audit Evidence</h2>
          <p>Evidence references remain visible to authorised operators.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="evidence-card">
        <div className="evidence-card__icon">Y</div>

        <div>
          <h3>YETI&apos;s governance interpretation</h3>

          <p>
            Governance evidence records what happened, who performed the
            action, why it occurred and when it occurred.
          </p>

          <p>
            YETI explains evidence for human review. An authorised human
            operator remains responsible for the decision.
          </p>

          {events.length > 0 && (
            <p className="evidence-card__event-count">
              {events.length} governance event
              {events.length === 1 ? "" : "s"} currently available.
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

import type { GovernanceStatus } from "../../services/governanceApi";

type Props = {
  data: GovernanceStatus;
};

function titleForComponent(component: string): string {
  const titles: Record<string, string> = {
    provider_management: "Provider Controls",
    cybersecurity_delivery: "Cybersecurity Governance",
  };

  return titles[component] ?? component.replaceAll("_", " ");
}

export default function GovernanceStatusPanel({ data }: Props) {
  const provider = data.controls.find(
    (control) => control.component === "provider_management",
  );

  const cybersecurity = data.controls.find(
    (control) => control.component === "cybersecurity_delivery",
  );

  const cards = [
    {
      key: "governance",
      eyebrow: "GOVERNANCE STATUS",
      title: data.recent_events.length ? "Attention" : "Healthy",
      description: data.recent_events.length
        ? "Recent governance activity has been recorded for operator review."
        : "No active governance alert has been recorded.",
      explanation: data.yeti.message,
      healthy: !data.recent_events.length,
    },
    {
      key: "provider",
      eyebrow: "PROVIDER CONTROLS",
      title: provider?.status === "paused" ? "Paused" : "Active",
      description:
        provider?.status === "paused"
          ? provider.details.reason ?? "Provider delivery is paused."
          : "Provider delivery is currently operating normally.",
      explanation:
        provider?.status === "paused"
          ? `YETI explains: ${provider.details.reason ?? "Provider delivery is paused."}`
          : "YETI explains: No provider pause is currently recorded.",
      healthy: provider?.status !== "paused",
    },
    {
      key: "cybersecurity",
      eyebrow: "CYBERSECURITY GOVERNANCE",
      title: cybersecurity?.status === "paused" ? "Paused" : "Monitored",
      description:
        cybersecurity?.status === "paused"
          ? cybersecurity.details.reason ?? "Cybersecurity delivery is paused."
          : "No cybersecurity delivery pause is currently recorded.",
      explanation:
        cybersecurity?.status === "paused"
          ? `YETI explains: ${cybersecurity.details.reason ?? "Cybersecurity delivery is paused."}`
          : "YETI explains: Cybersecurity signals are surfaced for governance review. Remediation remains outside YETI's authority.",
      healthy: cybersecurity?.status !== "paused",
    },
  ];

  return (
    <section className="governance-status-grid" aria-label="Governance status">
      {cards.map((card) => (
        <article className="governance-panel-card" key={card.key}>
          <div className="governance-panel-card__topline">
            <span className="governance-panel-card__eyebrow">
              {card.eyebrow}
            </span>

            <span
              className={`status-dot ${
                card.healthy ? "status-dot--healthy" : "status-dot--alert"
              }`}
              aria-label={card.healthy ? "Healthy" : "Attention required"}
            >
              ✓
            </span>
          </div>

          <h2>{card.title}</h2>
          <p className="governance-panel-card__description">
            {card.description}
          </p>

          <div className="yeti-explanation">
            <strong>YETI explains</strong>
            <span>{card.explanation}</span>
          </div>
        </article>
      ))}
    </section>
  );
}

                </small>

            </article>
          ))}

import type {
  PrivacyGovernanceCaseDetail as PrivacyGovernanceCase,
} from "../../lib/privacy-governance/types";

import PrivacyGovernanceDecisionActions from "./PrivacyGovernanceDecisionActions";
import styles from "./PrivacyGovernanceCaseDetail.module.css";

interface Props {
  open: boolean;
  loading: boolean;
  error: string | null;
  caseData: PrivacyGovernanceCase | null;
  onClose: () => void;
  onRetry: () => void;
  onDecisionComplete: (
    updatedCase: PrivacyGovernanceCase,
  ) => void;
}

function formatDate(value?: string | null): string {
  if (!value) {
    return "—";
  }

  const date = new Date(value);

  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
    return value;
  }

  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined, {
    dateStyle: "medium",
    timeStyle: "short",
  }).format(date);
}

function statusLabel(value?: string | null): string {
  if (!value) {
    return "Not yet decided";
  }

  return value.replaceAll("_", " ");
}

export function PrivacyGovernanceCaseDetail({
  open,
  loading,
  error,
  caseData,
  onClose,
  onRetry,
  onDecisionComplete,
}: Props) {
  if (!open) {
    return null;
  }

  /*
   * Backward-compatible evidence normalization.
   *
   * Some existing dashboard/API fixtures expose the evidence as
   * `approval_history`, while older fixtures use `approval_records`.
   * Both represent the same authoritative governance evidence.
   */
  const approvalHistory =
    caseData?.approval_history ??
    caseData?.approval_records ??
    [];

  const processingIds =
    caseData?.processing_ids ?? [];

  const dataMapIds =
    caseData?.data_map_ids ?? [];

  const trigger = caseData?.trigger;

  const isPending =
    caseData?.governance_status ===
    "PENDING_DPO_GRC";

  return (
    <div
      className={styles.backdrop}
      role="presentation"
      onMouseDown={onClose}
    >
      <aside
        className={styles.drawer}
        aria-label="Privacy governance case detail"
        onMouseDown={(event) =>
          event.stopPropagation()
        }
      >
        <header className={styles.header}>
          <div>
            <p className={styles.eyebrow}>
              CASE DETAIL
            </p>

            <h2>
              {caseData?.case_id ??
                "Privacy governance case"}
            </h2>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            className={styles.closeButton}
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="Close case detail"
          >
            ×
          </button>
        </header>

        {loading && (
          <div
            className={styles.state}
            role="status"
          >
            Loading case details…
          </div>
        )}

        {!loading && error && (
          <div
            className={styles.errorState}
            role="alert"
          >
            <strong>
              Unable to load this case.
            </strong>

            <span>{error}</span>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onRetry}
            >
              Retry
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        {!loading &&
          !error &&
          caseData && (
            <div className={styles.content}>
              <section
                className={styles.summaryCard}
              >
                <span>
                  Governance status
                </span>

                <strong
                  className={styles.status}
                >
                  {statusLabel(
                    caseData.governance_status,
                  )}
                </strong>
              </section>

              <section
                className={styles.section}
              >
                <h3>Overview</h3>

                <dl
                  className={styles.details}
                >
                  <div>
                    <dt>Case ID</dt>
                    <dd>
                      {caseData.case_id}
                    </dd>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <dt>Change ID</dt>
                    <dd>
                      {caseData.change_id || "—"}
                    </dd>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <dt>Title</dt>
                    <dd>
                      {caseData.title}
                    </dd>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <dt>Summary</dt>
                    <dd>
                      {caseData.summary}
                    </dd>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <dt>Trigger</dt>
                    <dd>
                      {trigger?.type
                        ? statusLabel(
                            trigger.type,
                          )
                        : "Not yet decided"}
                    </dd>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <dt>Trigger source</dt>
                    <dd>
                      {trigger?.source || "—"}
                    </dd>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <dt>Triggered</dt>
                    <dd>
                      {formatDate(
                        trigger?.at,
                      )}
                    </dd>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <dt>Risk</dt>
                    <dd>
                      {statusLabel(
                        caseData.risk,
                      )}
                    </dd>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <dt>Validation</dt>
                    <dd>
                      {statusLabel(
                        caseData.validation_status,
                      )}
                    </dd>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <dt>Email status</dt>
                    <dd>
                      {statusLabel(
                        caseData.email_status,
                      )}
                    </dd>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <dt>Final decision</dt>
                    <dd>
                      {statusLabel(
                        caseData.final_decision,
                      )}
                    </dd>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <dt>Decision time</dt>
                    <dd>
                      {formatDate(
                        caseData.final_decision_at,
                      )}
                    </dd>
                  </div>
                </dl>
              </section>

              <section
                className={styles.section}
              >
                <h3>Privacy scope</h3>

                <dl
                  className={styles.details}
                >
                  <div>
                    <dt>Processing IDs</dt>
                    <dd>
                      {processingIds.length
                        ? processingIds.join(", ")
                        : "None recorded"}
                    </dd>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <dt>Data mapping IDs</dt>
                    <dd>
                      {dataMapIds.length
                        ? dataMapIds.join(", ")
                        : "None recorded"}
                    </dd>
                  </div>
                </dl>
              </section>

              <section
                className={styles.section}
              >
                <div
                  className={
                    styles.sectionHeading
                  }
                >
                  <h3>
                    Approval evidence
                  </h3>

                  <span>
                    {approvalHistory.length} record
                    {approvalHistory.length === 1
                      ? ""
                      : "s"}
                  </span>
                </div>

                {approvalHistory.length > 0 ? (
                  <div
                    className={styles.approvals}
                  >
                    {approvalHistory.map(
                      (record) => (
                        <article
                          className={
                            styles.approval
                          }
                          key={
                            record.approval_id
                          }
                        >
                          <div
                            className={
                              styles.approvalTop
                            }
                          >
                            <strong>
                              {record.role}
                            </strong>

                            <span>
                              {statusLabel(
                                record.decision,
                              )}
                            </span>
                          </div>

                          <p>
                            {record.reviewer}
                          </p>

                          {record.comment && (
                            <blockquote>
                              {record.comment}
                            </blockquote>
                          )}

                          {record.decided_at && (
                            <small>
                              {formatDate(
                                record.decided_at,
                              )}
                            </small>
                          )}
                        </article>
                      ),
                    )}
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <p
                    className={styles.empty}
                  >
                    No approval evidence has
                    been recorded for this case.
                  </p>
                )}
              </section>

              {isPending && (
                <PrivacyGovernanceDecisionActions
                  caseData={caseData}
                  onDecisionComplete={
                    onDecisionComplete
                  }
                />
              )}
            </div>
          )}
      </aside>
    </div>
  );
}

export default PrivacyGovernanceCaseDetail;

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\frontend\components\privacy-governance\PrivacyGovernanceCaseEntry.tsx

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom";

import {
  getPrivacyGovernanceCase,
} from "../../lib/privacy-governance/api";

import type {
  PrivacyGovernanceCase,
} from "../../lib/privacy-governance/types";

import styles from "./PrivacyGovernanceCaseEntry.module.css";

function isCleared(
  governanceStatus?: string | null,
): boolean {
  return (
    governanceStatus?.toUpperCase() === "APPROVED"
  );
}

function statusLabel(
  value?: string | null,
): string {
  if (!value) {
    return "Unknown";
  }

  return value.replaceAll("_", " ");
}

export default function PrivacyGovernanceCaseEntry() {
  const { caseId } = useParams<{
    caseId?: string;
  }>();

  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [caseData, setCaseData] =
    useState<PrivacyGovernanceCase | null>(null);

  const [loading, setLoading] =
    useState(true);

  const [error, setError] =
    useState<string | null>(null);

  /*
   * ============================================================
   * LOAD AUTHORITATIVE CASE
   * ============================================================
   */

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;

    async function loadCase(): Promise<void> {
      if (!caseId) {
        setError(
          "Privacy governance case ID is missing.",
        );
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      setLoading(true);
      setError(null);

      try {
        const result =
          await getPrivacyGovernanceCase(caseId);

        if (!cancelled) {
          setCaseData(result);
        }
      } catch (cause) {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setError(
            cause instanceof Error
              ? cause.message
              : "Unable to load the privacy governance case.",
          );
        }
      } finally {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setLoading(false);
        }
      }
    }

    void loadCase();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [caseId]);

  /*
   * ============================================================
   * REDIRECT NON-CLEARED CASES
   *
   * IMPORTANT:
   * Navigation must happen inside useEffect, never during render.
   *
   * Existing dashboard remains the authoritative review surface.
   * ============================================================
   */

  useEffect(() => {
    if (
      !loading &&
      caseData &&
      !isCleared(caseData.governance_status)
    ) {
      navigate(
        `/privacy-governance?caseId=${encodeURIComponent(
          caseData.case_id,
        )}`,
        {
          replace: true,
        },
      );
    }
  }, [
    loading,
    caseData,
    navigate,
  ]);

  /*
   * ============================================================
   * LOADING
   * ============================================================
   */

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <main
        className={styles.page}
        aria-label="Privacy governance case"
      >
        <section
          className={styles.stateCard}
          role="status"
        >
          <p className={styles.eyebrow}>
            PRIVACY GOVERNANCE
          </p>

          <h1>
            Loading case...
          </h1>

          <p>
            Retrieving the authoritative governance
            record.
          </p>
        </section>
      </main>
    );
  }

  /*
   * ============================================================
   * ERROR
   * ============================================================
   */

  if (error || !caseData) {
    return (
      <main
        className={styles.page}
        aria-label="Privacy governance case"
      >
        <section
          className={styles.stateCard}
          role="alert"
        >
          <p className={styles.eyebrow}>
            PRIVACY GOVERNANCE
          </p>

          <h1>
            Unable to open case
          </h1>

          <p>
            {error ??
              "The requested governance case could not be found."}
          </p>

          <button
            type="button"
            className={styles.primaryButton}
            onClick={() =>
              navigate("/privacy-governance")
            }
          >
            Return to Governance Center
          </button>
        </section>
      </main>
    );
  }

  /*
   * ============================================================
   * NOT CLEARED
   *
   * The redirect is handled by the useEffect above.
   * Keep this render lightweight while navigation occurs.
   * ============================================================
   */

  if (!isCleared(caseData.governance_status)) {
    return (
      <main
        className={styles.page}
        aria-label="Privacy governance case"
      >
        <section
          className={styles.stateCard}
          role="status"
        >
          <p className={styles.eyebrow}>
            PRIVACY GOVERNANCE
          </p>

          <h1>
            Opening case review...
          </h1>

          <p>
            Opening the existing Governance Center
            review for this case.
          </p>
        </section>
      </main>
    );
  }

  /*
   * ============================================================
   * APPROVED
   *
   * Terminal success screen.
   *
   * This screen appears ONLY after the authoritative
   * governance record says APPROVED.
   * ============================================================
   */

  return (
    <main
      className={styles.page}
      aria-label="Privacy governance case cleared"
    >
      <section
        className={styles.clearedCard}
      >
        <p className={styles.eyebrow}>
          EASYCHATBOT · PRIVACY GOVERNANCE
        </p>

        <div
          className={styles.successIcon}
          aria-hidden="true"
        >
          ✓
        </div>

        <p className={styles.successLabel}>
          CASE CLEARED
        </p>

        <h1>
          Congratulations!
        </h1>

        <p className={styles.caseTitle}>
          {caseData.title}
        </p>

        <p className={styles.description}>
          This privacy governance case has
          been successfully reviewed and
          cleared.
        </p>

        <div className={styles.caseSummary}>
          <div>
            <span>Case ID</span>
            <strong>
              {caseData.case_id}
            </strong>
          </div>

          <div>
            <span>Change ID</span>
            <strong>
              {caseData.change_id || "—"}
            </strong>
          </div>

          <div>
            <span>Risk</span>
            <strong>
              {statusLabel(caseData.risk)}
            </strong>
          </div>

          <div>
            <span>Governance status</span>
            <strong>
              {statusLabel(
                caseData.governance_status,
              )}
            </strong>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className={styles.mascotArea}>
          <img
  src="/assets/mascot/welcome-sign-up-mascot.png"
  alt="EasyChatBot mascot"
  className={styles.mascot}
/>
        </div>

        <p className={styles.authoritativeNote}>
          The governance record remains the
          authoritative record for this case.
        </p>

        <button
          type="button"
          className={styles.secondaryButton}
          onClick={() =>
            navigate("/privacy-governance")
          }
        >
          Return to Governance Center
        </button>
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\frontend\components\privacy-governance\PrivacyGovernanceDashboard.tsx

import {
  useCallback,
  useEffect,
  useMemo,
  useState,
} from "react";

import PrivacyGovernanceCaseDetail from "./PrivacyGovernanceCaseDetail";
import styles from "./PrivacyGovernanceDashboard.module.css";

import {
  getPrivacyGovernanceCase,
  getPrivacyGovernanceCases,
  getPrivacyGovernanceStatusChart,
  getPrivacyGovernanceSummary,
} from "../../lib/privacy-governance/api";

import type {
  PrivacyGovernanceCase,
  PrivacyGovernanceStatusItem,
  PrivacyGovernanceSummary,
} from "../../lib/privacy-governance/types";

interface Props {
  initialCaseId?: string | null;
}

type CaseFilter =
  | "ALL"
  | "PENDING_DPO_GRC"
  | "APPROVED"
  | "REJECTED"
  | "BLOCKED";

function statusLabel(
  value?: string | null,
): string {
  if (!value) {
    return "Unknown";
  }

  return value.replaceAll("_", " ");
}

function formatDate(
  value?: string | null,
): string {
  if (!value) {
    return "—";
  }

  const date = new Date(value);

  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
    return value;
  }

  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined, {
    dateStyle: "medium",
    timeStyle: "short",
  }).format(date);
}

function matchesFilter(
  caseItem: PrivacyGovernanceCase,
  filter: CaseFilter,
): boolean {
  if (filter === "ALL") {
    return true;
  }

  return (
    caseItem.governance_status ===
    filter
  );
}

function normalizeStatusChart(
  value: PrivacyGovernanceStatusItem[] | null,
): PrivacyGovernanceStatusItem[] {
  if (!value) {
    return [];
  }

  return value.map((item) => ({
    status: item.status,
    count: Number(item.count ?? 0),
  }));
}

export default function PrivacyGovernanceDashboard({
  initialCaseId = null,
}: Props) {
  const [summary, setSummary] =
    useState<PrivacyGovernanceSummary | null>(
      null,
    );

  const [statusChart, setStatusChart] =
    useState<PrivacyGovernanceStatusItem[]>(
      [],
    );

  const [cases, setCases] = useState<
    PrivacyGovernanceCase[]
  >([]);

  const [filter, setFilter] =
    useState<CaseFilter>("ALL");

  const [selectedCaseId, setSelectedCaseId] =
    useState<string | null>(
      initialCaseId,
    );

  const [selectedCase, setSelectedCase] =
    useState<PrivacyGovernanceCase | null>(
      null,
    );

  const [detailLoading, setDetailLoading] =
    useState(false);

  const [detailError, setDetailError] =
    useState<string | null>(null);

  const [loading, setLoading] =
    useState(true);

  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] =
    useState(false);

  const [error, setError] =
    useState<string | null>(null);

  const loadDashboard = useCallback(
    async (
      refresh = false,
    ): Promise<void> => {
      if (refresh) {
        setRefreshing(true);
      } else {
        setLoading(true);
      }

      setError(null);

      try {
        const [
          summaryResult,
          statusResult,
          casesResult,
        ] = await Promise.all([
          getPrivacyGovernanceSummary(),
          getPrivacyGovernanceStatusChart(),
          getPrivacyGovernanceCases(),
        ]);

        setSummary(summaryResult);

        setStatusChart(
          normalizeStatusChart(
            statusResult,
          ),
        );

        setCases(casesResult);
      } catch (cause) {
        setError(
          cause instanceof Error
            ? cause.message
            : "Backend unavailable.",
        );
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
        setRefreshing(false);
      }
    },
    [],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    void loadDashboard();
  }, [loadDashboard]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setSelectedCaseId(
      initialCaseId ?? null,
    );
  }, [initialCaseId]);

  const loadCaseDetail = useCallback(
    async (
      caseId: string,
    ): Promise<void> => {
      setDetailLoading(true);
      setDetailError(null);

      try {
        const detail =
          await getPrivacyGovernanceCase(
            caseId,
          );

        setSelectedCase(detail);
      } catch (cause) {
        /*
         * Keep the dashboard usable if the
         * detail endpoint fails. The list
         * representation is still useful
         * fallback context.
         */
        const fallback =
          cases.find(
            (caseItem) =>
              caseItem.case_id ===
              caseId,
          ) ?? null;

        setSelectedCase(fallback);

        setDetailError(
          cause instanceof Error
            ? cause.message
            : "Unable to load case detail.",
        );
      } finally {
        setDetailLoading(false);
      }
    },
    [cases],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!selectedCaseId) {
      setSelectedCase(null);
      setDetailError(null);
      return;
    }

    void loadCaseDetail(
      selectedCaseId,
    );
  }, [
    selectedCaseId,
    loadCaseDetail,
  ]);

  const filteredCases = useMemo(
    () =>
      cases.filter((caseItem) =>
        matchesFilter(
          caseItem,
          filter,
        ),
      ),
    [cases, filter],
  );

  const totalCases =
    summary?.total_cases ??
    cases.length;

  const pendingCount =
    summary?.pending ??
    cases.filter(
      (caseItem) =>
        caseItem.governance_status ===
        "PENDING_DPO_GRC",
    ).length;

  const approvedCount =
    summary?.approved ??
    cases.filter(
      (caseItem) =>
        caseItem.governance_status ===
        "APPROVED",
    ).length;

  const blockedCount =
    summary?.blocked ??
    cases.filter(
      (caseItem) =>
        caseItem.governance_status ===
        "BLOCKED",
    ).length;

  const rejectedCount =
    cases.filter(
      (caseItem) =>
        caseItem.governance_status ===
        "REJECTED",
    ).length;

  const rejectedOrBlockedCount =
    rejectedCount +
    blockedCount;

  const approvalRecordCount =
    summary?.approval_records ??
    cases.reduce(
      (total, caseItem) =>
        total +
        (
          caseItem.approval_history ??
          []
        ).length,
      0,
    );

  const applyFilter = useCallback(
    (nextFilter: CaseFilter): void => {
      setFilter((current) =>
        current === nextFilter
          ? "ALL"
          : nextFilter,
      );
    },
    [],
  );

  const filterFromStatus =
    useCallback(
      (status: string): void => {
        const normalized =
          status.toUpperCase();

        if (
          normalized ===
          "PENDING_DPO_GRC"
        ) {
          applyFilter(
            "PENDING_DPO_GRC",
          );
          return;
        }

        if (
          normalized ===
          "APPROVED"
        ) {
          applyFilter("APPROVED");
          return;
        }

        if (
          normalized ===
          "REJECTED"
        ) {
          applyFilter("REJECTED");
          return;
        }

        if (
          normalized ===
          "BLOCKED"
        ) {
          applyFilter("BLOCKED");
        }
      },
      [applyFilter],
    );

  const openCase = useCallback(
    (caseId: string): void => {
      setSelectedCaseId(caseId);
    },
    [],
  );

  const closeCase = useCallback(
    (): void => {
      setSelectedCaseId(null);
      setSelectedCase(null);
      setDetailError(null);
    },
    [],
  );

  const handleDecisionComplete =
    useCallback(
      (
        updatedCase: PrivacyGovernanceCase,
      ): void => {
        setCases((current) =>
          current.map(
            (caseItem) =>
              caseItem.case_id ===
                updatedCase.case_id
                ? updatedCase
                : caseItem,
          ),
        );

        setSelectedCase(
          updatedCase,
        );
      },
      [],
    );

  if (
    loading &&
    !summary &&
    !error
  ) {
    return (
      <main
        className={styles.page}
        aria-label="Privacy governance dashboard"
      >
        <div
          className={styles.emptyState}
          role="status"
        >
          Loading privacy governance dashboard…
        </div>
      </main>
    );
  }

  return (
    <>
      <main
        className={styles.page}
        aria-label="Privacy governance dashboard"
      >
        {/* HEADER */}

        <header
          className={styles.header}
        >
          <div>
            <p
              className={styles.brand}
            >
              EASYCHATBOT · GOVERNANCE
            </p>

            <h1>
              Privacy Governance Center
            </h1>

            <p
              className={styles.subtitle}
            >
              Read-only oversight of
              privacy-impacting changes,
              validation and human
              approval evidence.
            </p>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            className={styles.refresh}
            onClick={() =>
              void loadDashboard(true)
            }
            disabled={refreshing}
          >
            {refreshing
              ? "Refreshing…"
              : "Refresh"}
          </button>
        </header>

        {/* ERROR */}

        {error && (
          <div
            className={
              styles.errorBanner
            }
            role="alert"
          >
            <strong>
              Unable to load governance data.
            </strong>

            <span>
              {error}
            </span>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() =>
                void loadDashboard()
              }
            >
              Retry
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* KPI CARDS */}

        <section
          className={styles.kpis}
          aria-label="Governance KPIs"
        >
          <button
            type="button"
            aria-label="Total cases KPI"
            className={`${styles.kpi} ${filter === "ALL"
                ? styles.kpiActive
                : ""
              }`}
            onClick={() =>
              applyFilter("ALL")
            }
          >
            <span>
              Total cases
            </span>

            <strong>
              {totalCases}
            </strong>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            aria-label="Pending DPO/GRC KPI"
            className={`${styles.kpi} ${filter ===
                "PENDING_DPO_GRC"
                ? styles.kpiActive
                : ""
              }`}
            onClick={() =>
              applyFilter(
                "PENDING_DPO_GRC",
              )
            }
          >
            <span>
              Pending DPO/GRC
            </span>

            <strong>
              {pendingCount}
            </strong>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            aria-label="Approved governance KPI"
            className={`${styles.kpi} ${filter === "APPROVED"
                ? styles.kpiActive
                : ""
              }`}
            onClick={() =>
              applyFilter("APPROVED")
            }
          >
            <span>
              Approved
            </span>

            <strong>
              {approvedCount}
            </strong>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            aria-label="Rejected / blocked KPI"
            className={`${styles.kpi} ${filter === "REJECTED" ||
                filter === "BLOCKED"
                ? styles.kpiActive
                : ""
              }`}
            onClick={() => {
              if (
                rejectedCount >
                0
              ) {
                applyFilter(
                  "REJECTED",
                );
              } else {
                applyFilter(
                  "BLOCKED",
                );
              }
            }}
          >
            <span>
              Rejected / blocked
            </span>

            <strong>
              {
                rejectedOrBlockedCount
              }
            </strong>
          </button>
        </section>

        {/* STATUS */}

        <section
          className={styles.panel}
          aria-label="Governance status"
        >
          <div
            className={
              styles.panelHeader
            }
          >
            <div>
              <p
                className={
                  styles.eyebrow
                }
              >
                OVERSIGHT
              </p>

              <h2>
                Governance status
              </h2>
            </div>

            <span>
              {approvalRecordCount}{" "}
              approval record
              {approvalRecordCount ===
                1
                ? ""
                : "s"}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div
            className={
              styles.statusList
            }
          >
            {statusChart.map(
              (item) => {
                const count =
                  Number(
                    item.count ??
                    0,
                  );

                const percentage =
                  totalCases > 0
                    ? Math.min(
                      100,
                      Math.max(
                        0,
                        (count /
                          totalCases) *
                        100,
                      ),
                    )
                    : 0;

                const label =
                  statusLabel(
                    item.status,
                  );

                /*
                 * IMPORTANT:
                 *
                 * The test contract expects
                 * the status and count to be
                 * present in the accessible
                 * name.
                 *
                 * Example:
                 * "APPROVED 1"
                 *
                 * This does not collide with
                 * the Approved KPI because its
                 * accessible name is
                 * "Approved governance KPI".
                 */
                return (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    key={
                      item.status
                    }
                    className={
                      styles.statusRow
                    }
                    aria-label={`${label} ${count}`}
                    onClick={() =>
                      filterFromStatus(
                        item.status,
                      )
                    }
                  >
                    <span>
                      {label}
                    </span>

                    <span
                      className={
                        styles.barTrack
                      }
                    >
                      <span
                        className={
                          styles.bar
                        }
                        style={{
                          width: `${percentage}%`,
                        }}
                      />
                    </span>

                    <strong>
                      {count}
                    </strong>
                  </button>
                );
              },
            )}
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* CASE REGISTER */}

        <section
          className={styles.panel}
          aria-label="Privacy governance cases"
        >
          <div
            className={
              styles.panelHeader
            }
          >
            <h2>
              Governance cases
            </h2>

            <div
              className={
                styles.caseMeta
              }
            >
              <span>
                {
                  filteredCases.length
                }{" "}
                case
                {filteredCases.length ===
                  1
                  ? ""
                  : "s"}
              </span>

              {filter !== "ALL" && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className={
                    styles.clearFilter
                  }
                  onClick={() =>
                    applyFilter(
                      "ALL",
                    )
                  }
                >
                  Clear filter
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          {filteredCases.length ===
            0 ? (
            <div
              className={
                styles.emptyState
              }
            >
              No governance cases
              match the selected
              filter.
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div
              className={
                styles.tableWrap
              }
            >
              <table
                className={
                  styles.table
                }
              >
                <thead>
                  <tr>
                    <th
                      className={
                        styles.headerCell
                      }
                    >
                      CASE ID
                    </th>

                    <th
                      className={
                        styles.headerCell
                      }
                    >
                      TITLE
                    </th>

                    <th
                      className={
                        styles.headerCell
                      }
                    >
                      STATUS
                    </th>

                    <th
                      className={
                        styles.headerCell
                      }
                    >
                      RISK
                    </th>

                    <th
                      className={
                        styles.headerCell
                      }
                    >
                      VALIDATION
                    </th>

                    <th
                      className={
                        styles.headerCell
                      }
                    >
                      TRIGGER
                    </th>

                    <th
                      className={
                        styles.headerCell
                      }
                    >
                      TRIGGERED
                    </th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>

                <tbody>
                  {filteredCases.map(
                    (caseItem) => (
                      <tr
                        key={
                          caseItem.case_id
                        }
                        className={
                          styles.row
                        }
                      >
                        <td
                          className={
                            styles.cell
                          }
                        >
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            className={
                              styles.caseLink
                            }
                            aria-label={caseItem.case_id}
                            title={`Open case ${caseItem.case_id}`}
                            onClick={() =>
                              openCase(
                                caseItem.case_id,
                              )
                            }
                          >
                            {
                              caseItem.case_id
                            }
                          </button>
                        </td>

                        <td
                          className={
                            styles.cell
                          }
                        >
                          {
                            caseItem.title
                          }
                        </td>

                        <td
                          className={
                            styles.cell
                          }

                          <span
                            className={
                              styles.statusPill
                            }
                          >
                            {statusLabel(
                              caseItem.governance_status,
                            )}
                          </span>
                        </td>

                        <td
                          className={
                            styles.cell
                          }

                            caseItem.risk,
                          )}

                        <td
                          className={
                            styles.cell
                          }
                        >
                          {statusLabel(
                            caseItem.validation_status,
                          )}
                        </td>

                        <td
                          className={
                            styles.cell
                          }
                        >
                          {statusLabel(
                            caseItem.trigger
                              ?.type,

                          }

                  )}

          )}
        </section>
      </main>

      {/* CASE DETAIL */}

      <PrivacyGovernanceCaseDetail
        open={
          selectedCaseId !== null
        }
        loading={detailLoading}
        error={detailError}

import {
  useMemo,
  useState,
} from "react";

import {
  submitGovernanceDecision,
  type GovernanceDecision,
  type GovernanceDecisionRequest,
  type GovernanceRole,
} from "../../lib/privacy-governance/decision-api";

import type {
  PrivacyGovernanceCaseDetail,
} from "../../lib/privacy-governance/types";

import styles from "./PrivacyGovernanceDecisionActions.module.css";

interface Props {
  /**
   * Preferred public prop name.
   */
  caseDetail?: PrivacyGovernanceCaseDetail;

  /**
   * Backward-compatible prop used by the
   * existing case-detail component.
   */
  caseData?: PrivacyGovernanceCaseDetail;

  /**
   * Preferred callback name.
   */
  onDecisionCompleted?: (
    updatedCase: PrivacyGovernanceCaseDetail,
  ) => void;

  /**
   * Backward-compatible callback name.
   */
  onDecisionComplete?: (
    updatedCase: PrivacyGovernanceCaseDetail,
  ) => void;
}

function createApprovalId(
  caseId: string,
  role: GovernanceRole,
): string {
  const randomUuid =
    typeof crypto !== "undefined" &&
    typeof crypto.randomUUID === "function"
      ? crypto.randomUUID()
      : `${Date.now()}-${Math.random()
          .toString(36)
          .slice(2, 10)}`;

  return `APR-UI-${role}-${caseId}-${randomUuid}`;
}

function statusLabel(
  value?: string | null,
): string {
  if (!value) {
    return "Unknown";
  }

  return value.replaceAll("_", " ");
}

function decisionLabel(
  decision: GovernanceDecision,
): string {
  switch (decision) {
    case "approve":
      return "Approve";

    case "reject":
      return "Reject";

    case "request-changes":
      return "Request changes";

    default:
      return "Decision";
  }
}

export function PrivacyGovernanceDecisionActions({
  caseDetail,
  caseData,
  onDecisionCompleted,
  onDecisionComplete,
}: Props) {
  /*
   * Resolve the case from the preferred or
   * backward-compatible prop.
   */
  const resolvedCase =
    caseDetail ?? caseData ?? null;

  /*
   * Resolve the preferred or backward-compatible
   * completion callback.
   */
  const handleDecisionCompleted =
    onDecisionCompleted ??
    onDecisionComplete;

  /*
   * Hooks must always execute in the same order.
   */
  const [role, setRole] =
    useState<GovernanceRole>("DPO");

  const [reviewer, setReviewer] =
    useState("");

  const [comment, setComment] =
    useState("");

  const [pendingDecision, setPendingDecision] =
    useState<GovernanceDecision | null>(
      null,
    );

  const [submitting, setSubmitting] =
    useState(false);

  const [error, setError] =
    useState<string | null>(null);

  /*
   * Generate the approval evidence identifier
   * for the current case and governance role.
   */
  const approvalId = useMemo(() => {
    if (!resolvedCase) {
      return "";
    }

    return createApprovalId(
      resolvedCase.case_id,
      role,
    );
  }, [
    resolvedCase?.case_id,
    role,
  ]);

  /*
   * Defensive integration state.
   */
  if (!resolvedCase) {
    return (
      <section className={styles.section}>
        <div
          className={styles.error}
          role="alert"
        >
          Privacy governance case data is unavailable.
        </div>
      </section>
    );
  }

  /*
   * From this point onward TypeScript knows that
   * resolvedCase exists.
   *
   * Create a stable local reference so nested
   * callbacks/functions also retain the narrowing.
   */
  const currentCase =
    resolvedCase;

  const isPending =
    currentCase.governance_status ===
    "PENDING_DPO_GRC";

  /*
   * Completed cases are read-only.
   */
  if (!isPending) {
    return (
      <section className={styles.section}>
        <div
          className={styles.completedCard}
        >
          <div>
            <span
              className={styles.eyebrow}
            >
              HUMAN GOVERNANCE DECISION
            </span>

            <strong>
              Governance decision recorded
            </strong>

            <p>
              This case is currently{" "}
              <b>
                {statusLabel(
                  currentCase.governance_status,
                )}
              </b>
              . No additional decision is
              available while the case is
              outside the pending governance
              state.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>
    );
  }

  const canSubmit =
    reviewer.trim().length > 0 &&
    comment.trim().length > 0 &&
    currentCase.change_id.trim().length > 0 &&
    Boolean(approvalId) &&
    !submitting;

  async function executeDecision(
    decision: GovernanceDecision,
  ): Promise<void> {
    /*
     * Prevent duplicate submissions.
     */
    if (submitting) {
      return;
    }

    /*
     * Validate the authoritative Change ID.
     */
    if (!currentCase.change_id.trim()) {
      setError(
        "This governance case has no linked privacy change. The decision cannot be submitted.",
      );
      return;
    }

    if (!reviewer.trim()) {
      setError(
        "Reviewer identity is required.",
      );
      return;
    }

    if (!comment.trim()) {
      setError(
        "A decision comment is required.",
      );
      return;
    }

    if (!approvalId) {
      setError(
        "Unable to create governance approval evidence identifier.",
      );
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    setError(null);

    const request: GovernanceDecisionRequest =
      {
        change_id:
          currentCase.change_id.trim(),

        approval_id:
          approvalId,

        reviewer:
          reviewer.trim(),

        role,

        comment:
          comment.trim(),
      };

    try {
      /*
       * The backend is authoritative.
       */
      const result =
        await submitGovernanceDecision(
          currentCase.case_id,
          decision,
          request,
        );

      /*
       * Only update the parent after successful
       * backend confirmation.
       */
      handleDecisionCompleted?.(
        result.case,
      );

      /*
       * Close confirmation only after success.
       */
      setPendingDecision(null);
      setError(null);
    } catch (cause) {
      /*
       * Keep confirmation workflow open after
       * API failure so the reviewer can retry.
       */
      setError(
        cause instanceof Error
          ? cause.message
          : "Governance decision failed.",
      );
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  function requestDecision(
    decision: GovernanceDecision,
  ): void {
    if (!canSubmit) {
      return;
    }

    setError(null);
    setPendingDecision(decision);
  }

  return (
    <section className={styles.section}>
      <div className={styles.heading}>
        <div>
          <span
            className={styles.eyebrow}
          >
            HUMAN GOVERNANCE DECISION
          </span>

          <h3>
            Decision required
          </h3>

          <p>
            This case passed automated
            validation and is awaiting
            explicit human governance
            approval.
          </p>
        </div>

        <span
          className={styles.pendingBadge}
        >
          PENDING DPO/GRC
        </span>
      </div>

      <div className={styles.card}>
        <div className={styles.field}>
          <label htmlFor="governance-role">
            Decision role
          </label>

          <select
            id="governance-role"
            value={role}
            onChange={(event) => {
              setRole(
                event.target
                  .value as GovernanceRole,
              );

              setPendingDecision(null);
              setError(null);
            }}
            disabled={submitting}
          >
            <option value="DPO">
              DPO
            </option>

            <option value="GRC">
              GRC
            </option>
          </select>
        </div>

        <div className={styles.field}>
          <label htmlFor="governance-reviewer">
            Reviewer
          </label>

          <input
            id="governance-reviewer"
            type="text"
            value={reviewer}
            onChange={(event) => {
              setReviewer(
                event.target.value,
              );

              if (error) {
                setError(null);
              }
            }}
            placeholder="Enter reviewer name"
            disabled={submitting}
            autoComplete="off"
          />
        </div>

        <div className={styles.field}>
          <label htmlFor="governance-comment">
            Decision rationale
          </label>

          <textarea
            id="governance-comment"
            value={comment}
            onChange={(event) => {
              setComment(
                event.target.value,
              );

              if (error) {
                setError(null);
              }
            }}
            placeholder="Record the rationale for this decision"
            rows={4}
            disabled={submitting}
          />
        </div>

        {!currentCase.change_id.trim() && (
          <div
            className={styles.warning}
            role="alert"
          >
            This case is missing its
            Change ID relationship. A
            governance decision is
            therefore blocked.
          </div>
        )}

        {error && (
          <div
            className={styles.error}
            role="alert"
          >
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        <div className={styles.actions}>
          <button
            type="button"
            className={`${styles.actionButton} ${styles.approve}`}
            disabled={!canSubmit}
            onClick={() =>
              requestDecision("approve")
            }
          >
            Approve as {role}
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            className={`${styles.actionButton} ${styles.reject}`}
            disabled={!canSubmit}
            onClick={() =>
              requestDecision("reject")
            }
          >
            Reject
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            className={`${styles.actionButton} ${styles.changes}`}
            disabled={!canSubmit}
            onClick={() =>
              requestDecision(
                "request-changes",
              )
            }
          >
            Request changes
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {pendingDecision && (
        <div
          className={styles.confirmBackdrop}
          role="presentation"
        >
          <div
            className={styles.confirmDialog}
            role="dialog"
            aria-modal="true"
            aria-labelledby="governance-confirm-title"
          >
            <span
              className={styles.eyebrow}
            >
              CONFIRM GOVERNANCE DECISION
            </span>

            <h3
              id="governance-confirm-title"
            >
              {decisionLabel(
                pendingDecision,
              )}
            </h3>

            <p>
              You are about to record a
              formal human governance
              decision for:
            </p>

            <dl
              className={
                styles.confirmDetails
              }
            >
              <div>
                <dt>Case</dt>

                <dd>
                  {currentCase.case_id}
                </dd>
              </div>

              <div>
                <dt>Change</dt>

                <dd>
                  {currentCase.change_id}
                </dd>
              </div>

              <div>
                <dt>Role</dt>

                <dd>
                  {role}
                </dd>
              </div>

              <div>
                <dt>Reviewer</dt>

                <dd>
                  {reviewer}
                </dd>
              </div>
            </dl>

            <p
              className={
                styles.confirmWarning
              }
            >
              This action will be recorded
              as governance evidence and
              may change the governance
              state of the case.
            </p>

            <div
              className={
                styles.confirmActions
              }
            >
              <button
                type="button"
                className={
                  styles.cancelButton
                }
                disabled={submitting}
                onClick={() => {
                  setPendingDecision(null);
                  setError(null);
                }}
              >
                Cancel
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                className={`${styles.confirmButton} ${
                  pendingDecision ===
                  "approve"
                    ? styles.approve
                    : pendingDecision ===
                        "reject"
                      ? styles.reject
                      : styles.changes
                }`}
                disabled={submitting}
                onClick={() =>
                  void executeDecision(
                    pendingDecision,
                  )
                }
              >
                {submitting
                  ? "Recording..."
                  : "Confirm decision"}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

export default PrivacyGovernanceDecisionActions;
