============================================================
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\frontend\src\agentic-cinema\CinemaApp.tsx
============================================================
import { Component, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";

import styles from "./CinemaApp.module.css";
import {
  runCinema,
  runCinemaAction,
  type CinemaResult,
} from "./cinemaApi";

type CinemaErrorBoundaryProps = {
  children: ReactNode;
};

type CinemaErrorBoundaryState = {
  hasError: boolean;
  message: string;
};

class CinemaErrorBoundary extends Component<
  CinemaErrorBoundaryProps,
  CinemaErrorBoundaryState
> {
  state: CinemaErrorBoundaryState = { hasError: false, message: "" };

  static getDerivedStateFromError(error: unknown): CinemaErrorBoundaryState {
    return {
      hasError: true,
      message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unknown Cinema render error.",
    };
  }

  componentDidCatch(error: unknown) {
    console.error("[Agentic Cinema] render error:", error);
  }

  render() {
    if (this.state.hasError) {
      return (
        <section style={{ padding: "48px", maxWidth: "900px", margin: "0 auto" }}>
          <h1>Agentic Cinema could not render this screen.</h1>
          <p>The Cinema application caught a UI rendering error instead of going blank.</p>
          <pre style={{ whiteSpace: "pre-wrap", overflowWrap: "anywhere" }}>{this.state.message}</pre>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => this.setState({ hasError: false, message: "" })}>
            Try again
          </button>
        </section>
      );
    }

    return this.props.children;
  }
}

type View =
  | "home"
  | "chat"
  | "results"
  | "challenge"
  | "academy"
  | "recommendations"
  | "profile";

const LUMINATE_IMAGE = "/characters/luminate/Luminate-template.png";

const prompts = [
  "What are the most important developments in the film industry this week?",
  "What are critics saying about the latest cinema trends?",
  "What streaming trends should filmmakers watch this month?",
  "Recommend a French-language film topic for me.",
  "Explain a current film-industry story in simple French.",
];

type TimedFlashcard = {
  id: string;
  provider: "Spotify" | "YouTube";
  eyebrow: string;
  title: string;
  message: string;
  cta: string;
  icon: string;
};

const CINEMA_FLASHCARDS: TimedFlashcard[] = [
  {
    id: "spotify-french-cinema",
    provider: "Spotify",
    eyebrow: "MEMORY PICK · 00:10",
    title: "French Cinema Soundtracks",
    message:
      "Your French B2 + cinema profile suggests a listening break: explore a soundtrack and practise vocabulary through music.",
    cta: "Explore Spotify picks",
    icon: "♫",
  },
  {
    id: "youtube-film-industry",
    provider: "YouTube",
    eyebrow: "MEMORY PICK · 00:25",
    title: "Film Industry & Streaming",
    message:
      "Your cinema and industry interests suggest a short video session. Explore current film-business conversations.",
    cta: "Explore YouTube picks",
    icon: "▶",
  },
];

export function CinemaApp() {
  const [view, setView] = useState<View>("home");
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [result, setResult] = useState<CinemaResult | null>(null);
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(
    () =>
      window.localStorage.getItem("easychatbot.cinema.saved") === "true",
  );
  const [actionMessage, setActionMessage] = useState("");
  const [profileOpen, setProfileOpen] = useState(false);
  const [notificationsOpen, setNotificationsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [flashcards, setFlashcards] = useState<TimedFlashcard[]>([]);
  const [activeFlashcard, setActiveFlashcard] =
    useState<TimedFlashcard | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const timers = CINEMA_FLASHCARDS.map((card, index) =>
      window.setTimeout(() => {
        setFlashcards((current) =>
          current.some((item) => item.id === card.id)
            ? current
            : [...current, card],
        );
        setActiveFlashcard(card);
      }, index === 0 ? 10_000 : 25_000),
    );

    return () => timers.forEach((timer) => window.clearTimeout(timer));
  }, []);

  function dismissFlashcard(id: string) {
    setActiveFlashcard((current) =>
      current?.id === id ? null : current,
    );
  }

  function openFlashcardRecommendations() {
    setActiveFlashcard(null);
    setNotificationsOpen(false);
    navigate("recommendations");
  }

  const intelligenceText = useMemo(() => {
    if (!result) {
      return "";
    }

    const candidate =
      result.intelligence ?? result.summary ?? result.result ?? "";

    if (typeof candidate === "string") {
      return candidate;
    }

    if (candidate == null) {
      return "";
    }

    try {
      return JSON.stringify(candidate, null, 2);
    } catch {
      return String(candidate);
    }
  }, [result]);

  async function executeCinema(question = message) {
    const clean = question.trim();

    if (!clean || loading) {
      return;
    }

    setMessage(clean);
    setLoading(true);
    setResult(null);
    setActionMessage("");

    // Keep the user on the stable Chat screen while the long-running
    // Agent Runtime workflow executes. Only transition to Results after
    // the request has completed, so there is never an empty intermediate
    // screen during the live Parallel + Gemini workflow.
    setView("chat");

    try {
      const data = await runCinema(clean);
      setResult(data);
      setLoading(false);
      setView("results");
    } catch (error) {
      setResult({
        success: false,
        error:
          error instanceof Error
            ? error.message
            : "Unable to reach the cinema service.",
      });
      setLoading(false);
      setView("results");
    }
  }

  function openChat(prefill = "") {
    setMessage(prefill);
    setResult(null);
    setActionMessage("");
    setView("chat");
  }

  function navigate(next: View) {
    setView(next);

    if (next !== "profile") {
      setProfileOpen(false);
    }
  }

  async function saveResult() {
    if (!result || saved) {
      return;
    }

    try {
      const response = await runCinemaAction("save", {
        user_id: "hackathon-judge",
        result,
      });

      if (response.success === false) {
        throw new Error(
          response.error || "The save service could not complete the action.",
        );
      }

      window.localStorage.setItem(
        "easychatbot.cinema.saved",
        "true",
      );

      setSaved(true);
      setActionMessage("Saved to your Cinema learning journey.");
    } catch {
      // Keep the demo journey functional even if the optional
      // persistence endpoint is unavailable.
      window.localStorage.setItem(
        "easychatbot.cinema.saved",
        "true",
      );

      setSaved(true);
      setActionMessage("Saved on this device.");
    }
  }

  function shareResult() {
    const text = intelligenceText.slice(0, 900);

    if (
      typeof navigator !== "undefined" &&
      typeof navigator.share === "function"
    ) {
      navigator
        .share({
          title: "EasyChatBot · Agentic Cinema",
          text,
        })
        .catch(() => undefined);

      return;
    }

    if (
      typeof navigator !== "undefined" &&
      navigator.clipboard
    ) {
      navigator.clipboard
        .writeText(text)
        .then(() => setActionMessage("Result copied to clipboard."))
        .catch(() => undefined);
    }
  }

  function renderView(): ReactNode {
    switch (view) {
      case "home":
        return (
          <HomeScreen
            onStart={() => openChat()}
            onPrompt={(prompt) => openChat(prompt)}
            onAcademy={() => navigate("academy")}
            onRecommendations={() => navigate("recommendations")}
          />
        );

      case "chat":
        return (
          <ChatScreen
            message={message}
            setMessage={setMessage}
            loading={loading}
            onRun={() => executeCinema()}
            onPrompt={(prompt) => setMessage(prompt)}
            onBack={() => navigate("home")}
          />
        );

      case "results":
        return (
          <ResultsScreen
            question={message}
            loading={loading}
            result={result}
            intelligenceText={intelligenceText}
            saved={saved}
            actionMessage={actionMessage}
            onBack={() => navigate("chat")}
            onChallenge={() => navigate("challenge")}
            onSave={saveResult}
            onShare={shareResult}
            onRunAgain={() => navigate("chat")}
          />
        );

      case "challenge":
        return (
          <ChallengeScreen
            result={result}
            onBack={() => navigate("results")}
          />
        );

      case "academy":
        return (
          <AcademyScreen
            saved={saved}
            onBack={() => navigate("home")}
          />
        );

      case "recommendations":
        return (
          <RecommendationsScreen
            onBack={() => navigate("home")}
          />
        );

      case "profile":
        return (
          <ProfileScreen
            onBack={() => navigate("home")}
          />
        );

      default:
        return (
          <HomeScreen
            onStart={() => openChat()}
            onPrompt={(prompt) => openChat(prompt)}
            onAcademy={() => navigate("academy")}
            onRecommendations={() => navigate("recommendations")}
          />
        );
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className={styles.shell}>
      <aside className={styles.sidebar}>
        <Brand />

        <nav
          className={styles.sideNav}
          aria-label="Primary navigation"
        >
          <NavButton
            active={view === "home"}
            icon="⌂"
            label="Home"
            onClick={() => (loading ? openChat() : navigate("home"))}
          />

          <NavButton
            active={
              view === "chat" ||
              view === "results" ||
              view === "challenge"
            }
            icon="○"
            label="Chat"
            onClick={() => openChat()}
          />

          <NavButton
            active={view === "home"}
            icon="✦"
            label="Agentic Cinema"
            onClick={() => navigate("home")}
            accent
          />

          <NavButton
            active={view === "academy"}
            icon="♜"
            label="Academy"
            onClick={() => navigate("academy")}
          />

          <NavButton
            active={view === "academy"}
            icon="◫"
            label="Progress"
            onClick={() => navigate("academy")}
          />

          <NavButton
            active={view === "academy"}
            icon="✧"
            label="Memory"
            onClick={() => navigate("academy")}
          />

          <NavButton
            active={view === "recommendations"}
            icon="☆"
            label="Recommendations"
            onClick={() => navigate("recommendations")}
          />

          <NavButton
            icon="♧"
            label="Notifications"
            badge={String(5 + flashcards.length)}
            onClick={() => setNotificationsOpen(true)}
          />

          <NavButton
            active={view === "profile"}
            icon="♙"
            label="Profile"
            onClick={() => navigate("profile")}
          />

          <NavButton
            icon="⚙"
            label="Settings"
            onClick={() => setProfileOpen(true)}
          />
        </nav>

        <ProfileMini onClick={() => navigate("profile")} />
      </aside>

      <main className={styles.main}>
        <header className={styles.topbar}>
          <button
            type="button"
            className={styles.mobileBrand}
            onClick={() => navigate("home")}
          >
            ✦ EasyChatBot
          </button>

          <div className={styles.topActions}>
            <button
              type="button"
              className={styles.academyPill}
              onClick={() => navigate("academy")}
            >
              ♜ Your Academy
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              className={styles.iconButton}
              aria-label="Notifications"
              onClick={() => setNotificationsOpen(true)}
            >
              ♧
              <span>{5 + flashcards.length}</span>
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              className={styles.avatarButton}
              onClick={() => navigate("profile")}
            >
              L
            </button>
          </div>
        </header>

        <div>
          <CinemaErrorBoundary>{renderView()}</CinemaErrorBoundary>
        </div>

        {notificationsOpen && (
          <div
            className={styles.modalBackdrop}
            role="presentation"
            onClick={() => setNotificationsOpen(false)}
          >
            <section
              className={styles.notificationPanel}
              role="dialog"
              aria-modal="true"
              aria-labelledby="notifications-title"
              onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
            >
              <div className={styles.modalHeader}>
                <div>
                  <span className={styles.eyebrow}>
                    ACADEMY
                  </span>

                  <h2 id="notifications-title">
                    Notifications
                  </h2>
                </div>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setNotificationsOpen(false)}
                  className={styles.closeButton}
                  aria-label="Close notifications"
                >
                  ×
                </button>
              </div>

              <div className={styles.notificationItem}>
                <span>✦</span>

                <div>
                  <strong>New Cinema Mission</strong>

                  <p>
                    Run a live intelligence workflow and turn
                    the result into French practice.
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className={styles.notificationItem}>
                <span>♜</span>

                <div>
                  <strong>Academy Progress</strong>

                  <p>
                    Your B2 cinema-learning journey is ready
                    for the next step.
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className={styles.notificationItem}>
                <span>✧</span>

                <div>
                  <strong>Memory</strong>

                  <p>
                    Save a completed intelligence result to
                    continue your personalised journey.
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              {flashcards.map((card) => (
                <button
                  key={card.id}
                  type="button"
                  className={styles.notificationRecommendation}
                  onClick={openFlashcardRecommendations}
                >
                  <span>{card.icon}</span>
                  <div>
                    <strong>{card.title}</strong>
                    <p>
                      {card.provider} recommendation · personalised
                      from your Cinema profile.
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  <b>→</b>
                </button>
              ))}

              <button
                type="button"
                className={styles.primaryButtonSmall}
                onClick={() => {
                  setNotificationsOpen(false);
                  openChat();
                }}
              >
                Start a Mission →
              </button>
            </section>
          </div>
        )}

        {activeFlashcard && (
          <FlashcardToast
            card={activeFlashcard}
            onOpen={openFlashcardRecommendations}
            onDismiss={() => dismissFlashcard(activeFlashcard.id)}
          />
        )}

        {profileOpen && (
          <div className={styles.toast}>
            <strong>Settings</strong>

            <span>
              Configuration remains in the application/backend
              layer.
            </span>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setProfileOpen(false)}
            >
              Close
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </main>

      <MobileNav
        view={view}
        onNavigate={navigate}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

function FlashcardToast({
  card,
  onOpen,
  onDismiss,
}: {
  card: TimedFlashcard;
  onOpen: () => void;
  onDismiss: () => void;
}) {
  return (
    <aside
      className={styles.flashcardToast}
      role="status"
      aria-live="polite"
    >
      <div className={styles.flashcardVisual}>
        <CharacterAvatar large />
        <span aria-hidden="true">{card.icon}</span>
      </div>

      <div className={styles.flashcardContent}>
        <span className={styles.flashcardEyebrow}>
          {card.eyebrow}
        </span>

        <strong>{card.title}</strong>

        <p>{card.message}</p>

        <div className={styles.flashcardActions}>
          <button
            type="button"
            className={styles.primaryButtonSmall}
            onClick={onOpen}
          >
            {card.cta}
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            className={styles.flashcardDismiss}
            onClick={onDismiss}
          >
            Later
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <button
        type="button"
        className={styles.flashcardClose}
        onClick={onDismiss}
        aria-label="Dismiss recommendation"
      >
        ×
      </button>
    </aside>
  );
}

function ChatScreen({
  message,
  setMessage,
  loading,
  onRun,
  onPrompt,
  onBack,
}: {
  message: string;
  setMessage: (value: string) => void;
  loading: boolean;
  onRun: () => void;
  onPrompt: (prompt: string) => void;
  onBack: () => void;
}) {
  return (
    <section className={styles.contentNarrow}>
      <button
        type="button"
        className={styles.backButton}
        onClick={onBack}
        disabled={loading}
        aria-disabled={loading}
      >
        {loading ? "Research in progress…" : "← Back"}
      </button>

      <div className={styles.pageTitle}>
        <span className={styles.agentIcon}>✦</span>

        <div>
          <span className={styles.eyebrow}>
            AGENTIC CINEMA
          </span>

          <h1>
            Ask your Cinema Intelligence Agent
          </h1>
        </div>
      </div>

      {loading ? (
        <div className={styles.loadingCard} aria-live="polite">
          <div className={styles.spinner} />

          <h2>Your research crew is working…</h2>

          <p>
            Searching live sources with Parallel, analysing with Gemini,
            and preparing your personalised learning result.
          </p>

          <div className={styles.loadingSteps}>
            <span>✓ Live research</span>
            <span>✓ Gemini analysis</span>
            <span>◌ Personalisation</span>
          </div>

          <p>
            You can stay on this screen. The result will appear automatically
            when the workflow is complete.
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className={styles.chatCard}>
          <div className={styles.messageAgent}>
          <CharacterAvatar />

          <div>
            <strong>
              Luminate · Cinema Intelligence
            </strong>

            <p>
              Hi! I’m your Cinema Intelligence Agent.
              Ask me anything about movies, TV, the film
              industry, or a current story you want to
              understand.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className={styles.promptList}>
          {prompts.map((prompt) => (
            <button
              type="button"
              key={prompt}
              onClick={() => onPrompt(prompt)}
            >
              {prompt}
              <span>→</span>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className={styles.composer}>
          <textarea
            value={message}
            onChange={(event) =>
              setMessage(event.target.value)
            }
            onKeyDown={(event) => {
              if (
                event.key === "Enter" &&
                !event.shiftKey
              ) {
                event.preventDefault();
                onRun();
              }
            }}
            placeholder="Ask anything about movies, TV, or the industry…"
            rows={3}
          />

          <button
            type="button"
            className={styles.sendButton}
            onClick={onRun}
            disabled={loading || !message.trim()}
            aria-label="Send question"
          >
            {loading ? "…" : "➤"}
          </button>
        </div>

          <div className={styles.trustLine}>
            Secure application boundary · Gemini reasoning ·
            Parallel live research · No browser-side secrets
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

function ResultsScreen({
  question,
  loading,
  result,
  intelligenceText,
  saved,
  actionMessage,
  onBack,
  onChallenge,
  onSave,
  onShare,
  onRunAgain,
}: {
  question: string;
  loading: boolean;
  result: CinemaResult | null;
  intelligenceText: string;
  saved: boolean;
  actionMessage: string;
  onBack: () => void;
  onChallenge: () => void;
  onSave: () => void;
  onShare: () => void;
  onRunAgain: () => void;
}) {
  return (
    <section className={styles.contentNarrow}>
      <button
        type="button"
        className={styles.backButton}
        onClick={onBack}
      >
        ← Ask another question
      </button>

      <div className={styles.pageTitle}>
        <span className={styles.agentIcon}>✦</span>

        <div>
          <span className={styles.eyebrow}>
            AGENTIC CINEMA · RESULT
          </span>

          <h1>
            Your personalised cinema intelligence
          </h1>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className={styles.statusBar}>
        <span>● Parallel</span>
        <span>● Gemini</span>
        <span>● Memory</span>
        <span>● Personalisation</span>
      </div>

      {loading && (
        <div
          className={styles.loadingCard}
          aria-live="polite"
        >
          <div className={styles.spinner} />

          <h2>
            Your research crew is working…
          </h2>

          <p>
            Searching live sources with Parallel,
            analysing with Gemini, and preparing your
            personalised learning result.
          </p>

          <div className={styles.loadingSteps}>
            <span>✓ Live research</span>
            <span>✓ Gemini analysis</span>
            <span>◌ Personalisation</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {!loading && result?.error && (
        <div className={styles.errorCard}>
          <strong>
            The cinema service could not complete the
            request.
          </strong>

          <p>{result.error}</p>

          <button
            type="button"
            className={styles.primaryButtonSmall}
            onClick={onRunAgain}
          >
            Try Again
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {!loading && result && !result.error && (
        <article className={styles.resultCard}>
          <div className={styles.resultHeader}>
            <div>
              <span className={styles.resultTag}>
                LIVE INTELLIGENCE
              </span>

              <h2>
                {question || "Cinema Intelligence"}
              </h2>

              <small>
                Generated from the connected application
                workflow
              </small>
            </div>

            <span className={styles.liveBadge}>
              LIVE
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className={styles.resultBody}>
            {intelligenceText
              .split(/\n+/)
              .filter(Boolean)
              .map((line, index) => (
                <p key={`${index}-${line}`}>
                  {line}
                </p>
              ))}
          </div>

          {result.sources &&
            result.sources.length > 0 && (
              <div className={styles.sources}>
                <h3>Research sources</h3>

                {result.sources.map((source, index) => (
                  <a
                    key={`${source.title}-${index}`}
                    href={source.url || "#"}
                    target="_blank"
                    rel="noreferrer"
                  >
                    <span>{index + 1}</span>

                    <strong>{source.title}</strong>

                    <small>
                      {source.snippet ||
                        "Live source used by the workflow"}
                    </small>

                    →
                  </a>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}

          <div className={styles.resultActions}>
            <button
              type="button"
              className={styles.primaryButtonSmall}
              onClick={onChallenge}
            >
              Turn this into a learning challenge →
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              className={styles.secondaryButtonSmall}
              onClick={onSave}
            >
              {saved
                ? "✓ Saved on this device"
                : "☆ Save on this device"}
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              className={styles.secondaryButtonSmall}
              onClick={() => window.print()}
            >
              PDF / Print
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              className={styles.secondaryButtonSmall}
              onClick={onShare}
            >
              Share
            </button>
          </div>

          {actionMessage && (
            <div
              className={styles.actionMessage}
              role="status"
            >
              {actionMessage}
            </div>
          )}
        </article>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

function ChallengeScreen({
  result,
  onBack,
}: {
  result: CinemaResult | null;
  onBack: () => void;
}) {
  const challenge = result?.learning_challenge;

  const [answer, setAnswer] = useState("");
  const [checked, setChecked] = useState(false);
  const [checking, setChecking] = useState(false);
  const [feedback, setFeedback] = useState("");
  const [hintOpen, setHintOpen] = useState(false);

  async function checkAnswer() {
    if (!answer.trim()) {
      return;
    }

    setChecking(true);

    try {
      const response = await runCinemaAction(
        "challenge",
        {
          user_id: "hackathon-judge",
          answer,
          challenge: challenge ?? null,
        },
      );

      setChecked(true);

      setFeedback(
        response.feedback ||
          (response.success
            ? "Answer evaluated by the connected application."
            : "Evaluation endpoint is not configured yet."),
      );
    } catch {
      setChecked(true);
      setFeedback(
        "The evaluation service could not be reached.",
      );
    } finally {
      setChecking(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <section className={styles.contentNarrow}>
      <button
        type="button"
        className={styles.backButton}
        onClick={onBack}
      >
        ← Back to intelligence
      </button>

      <div className={styles.pageTitle}>
        <span className={styles.agentIcon}>☷</span>

        <div>
          <span className={styles.eyebrow}>
            LEARNING CHALLENGE · B2
          </span>

          <h1>
            Turn real-world insight into progress
          </h1>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className={styles.challengeCard}>
        <div className={styles.challengeHeader}>
          <span>✦ Learning Challenge</span>
          <b>B1 · B2</b>
        </div>

        <p className={styles.challengeIntro}>
          Based on today's live intelligence, practise
          your French with the real content you just
          explored.
        </p>

        <div className={styles.task}>
          <span>▣ TASK</span>

          <h2>
            {challenge?.prompt ||
              "Summarise the main development in French in 4–6 sentences."}
          </h2>

          <textarea
            value={answer}
            onChange={(event) =>
              setAnswer(event.target.value)
            }
            placeholder="Write your answer in French…"
            maxLength={500}
          />

          <div className={styles.answerMeta}>
            {answer.length}/500
          </div>

          <div className={styles.challengeActions}>
            <button
              type="button"
              className={styles.primaryButtonSmall}
              onClick={checkAnswer}
              disabled={
                checking || !answer.trim()
              }
            >
              {checking
                ? "Checking…"
                : "Check My Answer →"}
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              className={styles.secondaryButtonSmall}
              onClick={() =>
                setHintOpen((value) => !value)
              }
            >
              💡 {hintOpen ? "Hide hint" : "Get a hint"}
            </button>
          </div>

          {hintOpen && (
            <div className={styles.hint}>
              💡 Try using:{" "}
              {(
                challenge?.vocabulary || [
                  "production",
                  "industrie",
                  "innovation",
                ]
              )
                .slice(0, 3)
                .join(" · ")}
              .
            </div>
          )}

          {checked && (
            <div className={styles.feedback}>
              ✓ {feedback || "Answer received."}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className={styles.vocabulary}>
          <h3>Useful vocabulary</h3>

          <div>
            {(
              challenge?.vocabulary || [
                "festival",
                "catégorie",
                "production",
                "industrie",
                "innovation",
                "créativité",
              ]
            ).map((word) => (
              <span key={word}>{word}</span>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}


type Recommendation = {
  title: string;
  subtitle: string;
  reason: string;
  tags: string[];
  kind: "spotify" | "youtube";
  query: string;
};

function RecommendationsScreen({
  onBack,
}: {
  onBack: () => void;
}) {
  function openRecommendation(item: Recommendation) {
    const encoded = encodeURIComponent(item.query);
    const url =
      item.kind === "spotify"
        ? `https://open.spotify.com/search/${encoded}`
        : `https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=${encoded}`;

    window.open(url, "_blank", "noopener,noreferrer");
  }

  return (
    <section className={styles.content}>
      <button
        type="button"
        className={styles.backButton}
        onClick={onBack}
      >
        ← Back to Cinema
      </button>

      <div className={styles.pageTitle}>
        <span className={styles.agentIcon}>☆</span>

        <div>
          <span className={styles.eyebrow}>
            MEMORY-INFORMED RECOMMENDATIONS
          </span>

          <h1>Your Cinema Shelf</h1>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className={styles.recommendationIntro}>
        <div>
          <strong>Picked for your learning journey.</strong>
          <p>
            Your current profile combines French B2 learning with
            cinema, film-industry, streaming and cultural interests.
            These recommendations turn those interests into listening
            and viewing practice.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className={styles.recommendationMemory}>
          <span>✧ MEMORY</span>
          <strong>French · B2 · Cinema Explorer</strong>
          <small>
            Personalisation is based on the learner profile currently
            available to the Cinema experience.
          </small>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className={styles.recommendationSection}>
        <div className={styles.sectionHeading}>
          <div>
            <span className={styles.eyebrow}>LISTEN</span>
            <h2>Spotify recommendations</h2>
          </div>
          <span className={styles.recommendationProvider}>
            Spotify search
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className={styles.recommendationGrid}>
          {spotifyRecommendations.map((item) => (
            <RecommendationCard
              key={item.title}
              item={item}
              onOpen={() => openRecommendation(item)}
            />
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className={styles.recommendationSection}>
        <div className={styles.sectionHeading}>
          <div>

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import styles from "./CinemaShop.module.css";

export type ShopCategory = "all" | "services" | "trips" | "goods" | "other";

export type ShopRecommendation = {
  id: string;
  category: Exclude<ShopCategory, "all">;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  reason: string;
  externalUrl: string;
  sourceLabel: string;
  tags: string[];
};

const FALLBACK_RECOMMENDATIONS: ShopRecommendation[] = [
  {
    id: "french-market",
    category: "trips",
    title: "French Market Experience",
    description: "A relaxed cultural outing that can turn a French practice session into a real-world experience.",
    reason: "Suggested because it connects your French learning with your Cinema interests.",
    externalUrl: "https://www.google.com/search?q=French+market+experience",
    sourceLabel: "Explore externally",
    tags: ["French", "Culture", "Weekend"],
  },
  {
    id: "cinema-workshop",
    category: "services",
    title: "Screenwriting Workshop",
    description: "A workshop-style service for developing scenes, dialogue and story structure.",
    reason: "Fits a learner who enjoys creative and professional media topics.",
    externalUrl: "https://www.google.com/search?q=screenwriting+workshop",
    sourceLabel: "Explore externally",
    tags: ["Writing", "Cinema", "Learning"],
  },
  {
    id: "language-notebook",
    category: "goods",
    title: "French Vocabulary Notebook",
    description: "A simple physical notebook for collecting expressions, phrases and ideas from your Cinema sessions.",
    reason: "Useful for turning recommendations into lightweight learning habits.",
    externalUrl: "https://www.google.com/search?q=French+vocabulary+notebook",
    sourceLabel: "Explore externally",
    tags: ["French", "Study"],
  },
  {
    id: "festival-guide",
    category: "other",
    title: "Cinema Festival Guide",
    description: "Browse current festival programmes and discover something that catches your interest.",
    reason: "A low-pressure discovery option for your current media interests.",
    externalUrl: "https://www.google.com/search?q=film+festival+guide",
    sourceLabel: "Explore externally",
    tags: ["Film", "Discovery"],
  },
];

function normaliseRecommendations(payload: unknown): ShopRecommendation[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(payload)) return [];
  return payload.flatMap((item, index) => {
    if (!item || typeof item !== "object") return [];
    const value = item as Record<string, unknown>;
    const category = String(value.category ?? "other").toLowerCase();
    const safeCategory =
      category === "services" || category === "trips" || category === "goods"
        ? category
        : "other";
    const externalUrl = String(
      value.external_url ?? value.externalUrl ?? value.url ?? "",
    ).trim();
    if (!externalUrl || !/^https?:\/\//i.test(externalUrl)) return [];
    return [{
      id: String(value.id ?? `shop-${index}`),
      category: safeCategory as Exclude<ShopCategory, "all">,
      title: String(value.title ?? "Explore this recommendation"),
      description: String(value.description ?? "A recommendation selected for discovery."),
      reason: String(value.reason ?? "Suggested for your current interests."),
      externalUrl,
      sourceLabel: String(value.source_label ?? value.sourceLabel ?? "Explore externally"),
      tags: Array.isArray(value.tags) ? value.tags.map(String) : [],
    }];
  });
}

async function loadRecommendations(): Promise<ShopRecommendation[]> {
  try {
    const response = await fetch("/hackathon/flash-deal", {
      headers: { Accept: "application/json" },
    });
    if (!response.ok) return [];
    const payload = await response.json();
    return normaliseRecommendations(payload.items ?? payload.recommendations ?? payload);
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

export default function CinemaShop() {
  const [category, setCategory] = useState<ShopCategory>("all");
  const [items, setItems] = useState<ShopRecommendation[]>(FALLBACK_RECOMMENDATIONS);

  useEffect(() => {
    let mounted = true;
    void loadRecommendations().then((remoteItems) => {
      if (mounted && remoteItems.length > 0) setItems(remoteItems);
    });
    return () => { mounted = false; };
  }, []);

  const filtered = useMemo(
    () => category === "all" ? items : items.filter((item) => item.category === category),
    [category, items],
  );

  return (
    <section className={styles.shopPage} aria-labelledby="cinema-shop-title">
      <header className={styles.shopHeader}>
        <div>
          <p className={styles.eyebrow}>EASYCHATBOT · DISCOVER</p>
          <h1 id="cinema-shop-title">Shop &amp; Discover</h1>
          <p className={styles.subtitle}>
            A few things that may fit your interests. Browse casually — there is no need to buy anything here.
          </p>
        </div>
      </header>

      <div className={styles.categoryBar} role="tablist" aria-label="Shop categories">
        {([
          ["all", "For you"],
          ["services", "Services"],
          ["trips", "Trips"],
          ["goods", "Goods"],
          ["other", "Other"],
        ] as const).map(([value, label]) => (
          <button
            key={value}
            type="button"
            role="tab"
            aria-selected={category === value}
            className={category === value ? styles.categoryActive : styles.category}
            onClick={() => setCategory(value)}
          >
            {label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className={styles.discoveryNote}>
        <strong>Personalised discovery</strong>
        <span>Explore something interesting and continue on the actual website.</span>
      </div>

      <div className={styles.grid}>
        {filtered.map((item) => (
          <article className={styles.card} key={item.id}>
            <span className={styles.categoryLabel}>{item.category}</span>
            <h2>{item.title}</h2>
            <p className={styles.description}>{item.description}</p>
            <p className={styles.reason}>{item.reason}</p>
            <div className={styles.tags}>
              {item.tags.map((tag) => <span key={tag}>{tag}</span>)}
            </div>
            <a
              className={styles.exploreButton}
              href={item.externalUrl}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
            >
              Explore website <span aria-hidden="true">↗</span>
            </a>
            <span className={styles.externalHint}>{item.sourceLabel}</span>
          </article>
        ))}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\frontend\src\auth\authApi.ts
============================================================
export interface LoginRequest {
  email: string;
  password: string;
}

export interface LoginResponse {
  detail: string;
  expires_at: string;
}

export interface SessionResponse {
  authenticated: boolean;
}

export class AuthenticationApiError extends Error {
  readonly status: number;

  constructor(
    message: string,
    status: number,
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "AuthenticationApiError";
    this.status = status;
  }
}

export async function getSession(): Promise<SessionResponse> {
  const response = await fetch("/auth/session", {
    method: "GET",
    credentials: "include",
    headers: {
      Accept: "application/json",
    },
  });

  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail = `Authentication session check failed (${response.status}).`;

    try {
      const body = await response.json();

      if (
        body &&
        typeof body.detail === "string"
      ) {
        detail = body.detail;
      }
    } catch {
      // Preserve safe generic API error.
    }

    throw new AuthenticationApiError(
      detail,
      response.status,
    );
  }

  return response.json() as Promise<SessionResponse>;
}

export async function login(
  credentials: LoginRequest,
): Promise<LoginResponse> {
  const response = await fetch("/auth/login", {
    method: "POST",
    credentials: "include",
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": "application/json",
      Accept: "application/json",
    },
    body: JSON.stringify(credentials),
  });

  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail = `Authentication failed (${response.status}).`;

    try {
      const body = await response.json();

      if (
        body &&
        typeof body.detail === "string"
      ) {
        detail = body.detail;
      }
    } catch {
      // Preserve safe generic error.
    }

    throw new AuthenticationApiError(
      detail,
      response.status,
    );
  }

  return response.json() as Promise<LoginResponse>;
}

export async function logout(): Promise<void> {
  const response = await fetch("/auth/logout", {
    method: "POST",
    credentials: "include",
  });

  if (!response.ok && response.status !== 204) {
    throw new AuthenticationApiError(
      `Logout failed (${response.status}).`,
      response.status,
    );
  }
}

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\frontend\src\auth\AuthContext.ts
============================================================
import { createContext } from "react";

import type { LoginRequest } from "./authApi";

export type AuthStatus =
  | "unknown"
  | "authenticated"
  | "unauthenticated";

export interface AuthContextValue {
  status: AuthStatus;
  login: (credentials: LoginRequest) => Promise<void>;
  logout: () => Promise<void>;
  refresh: () => Promise<void>;
}

export const AuthContext =
  createContext<AuthContextValue | null>(null);

if (import.meta.env.DEV) {
  const existing = (
    window as typeof window & {
      __easychatbot_auth_context__?: typeof AuthContext;
    }
  ).__easychatbot_auth_context__;

  if (existing && existing !== AuthContext) {
    console.error(
      "[EasyChatBot Auth] DUPLICATE AuthContext detected",
      {
        existing,
        current: AuthContext,
        same: existing === AuthContext,
      },
    );
  }

  (
    window as typeof window & {
      __easychatbot_auth_context__?: typeof AuthContext;
    }
  ).__easychatbot_auth_context__ = AuthContext;
}

============================================================
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\frontend\src\auth\AuthProvider.tsx
============================================================
import {
  useCallback,
  useMemo,
  useState,
  type ReactNode,
} from "react";

import {
  getSession,
  login as loginRequest,
  logout as logoutRequest,
  type LoginRequest,
} from "./authApi";

import { AuthContext } from "./AuthContext";

export function AuthProvider({
  children,
}: {
  children: ReactNode;
}) {
  const [status, setStatus] =
    useState<
      "unknown" |
      "authenticated" |
      "unauthenticated"
    >("unknown");

  const refresh = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const session = await getSession();

      if (session.authenticated) {
        setStatus("authenticated");
      } else {
        setStatus("unauthenticated");
      }
    } catch (error) {
      /*
       * A 401 is an expected unauthenticated state.
       *
       * Unexpected API/network failures are fail-closed for
       * frontend navigation. The backend remains the authoritative
       * security boundary.
       */
      setStatus("unauthenticated");

      throw error;
    }
  }, []);

  const login = useCallback(
    async (credentials: LoginRequest) => {
      await loginRequest(credentials);

      /*
       * The backend has now established its existing
       * HttpOnly easychatbot_session cookie.
       *
       * No credential/session value is stored in browser
       * application storage.
       */
      setStatus("authenticated");
    },
    [],
  );

  const logout = useCallback(async () => {
    await logoutRequest();

    setStatus("unauthenticated");
  }, []);

  const value = useMemo(
    () => ({
      status,
      login,
      logout,
      refresh,
    }),
    [
      status,
      login,
      logout,
      refresh,
    ],
  );

  /*
   * TEMPORARY DEVELOPMENT DIAGNOSTIC
   *
   * This allows us to determine whether Vite is loading
   * multiple AuthContext module instances.
   *
   * This must be removed after the root cause is confirmed.
   */
  if (import.meta.env.DEV) {
    const diagnosticWindow =
      window as typeof window & {
        __easychatbot_auth_context__?: typeof AuthContext;
      };

    console.log(
      "[EasyChatBot Auth] AuthProvider rendering",
      {
        status,
        context:
          diagnosticWindow.__easychatbot_auth_context__,
        sameContext:
          diagnosticWindow.__easychatbot_auth_context__ ===
          AuthContext,
      },
    );
  }

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider value={value}>
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
}

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\frontend\src\auth\ProtectedRoute.tsx
============================================================
import {
  Navigate,
  useLocation,
} from "react-router-dom";

import {
  useEffect,
  useState,
  type ReactNode,
} from "react";

import { useAuth } from "./useAuth";

export default function ProtectedRoute({
  children,
}: {
  children: ReactNode;
}) {
  const {
    status,
    refresh,
  } = useAuth();

  const location = useLocation();

  const [checking, setChecking] = useState(
    status === "unknown",
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (status !== "unknown") {
      setChecking(false);
      return;
    }

    let active = true;

    void refresh()
      .catch(() => {
        if (active) {
          /*
           * Fail closed from the frontend navigation
           * perspective. Backend authorization remains
           * authoritative.
           */
        }
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (active) {
          setChecking(false);
        }
      });

    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [refresh, status]);

  if (checking) {
    return (
      <main aria-label="Authentication check">
        Checking access�
      </main>
    );
  }

  if (status !== "authenticated") {
    const redirect = encodeURIComponent(
      `${location.pathname}${location.search}`,
    );

    return (
      <Navigate
        to={`/login?redirect=${redirect}`}
        replace
      />
    );
  }

  return <>{children}</>;
}

============================================================
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\frontend\src\auth\useAuth.ts
============================================================
import { useContext } from "react";

import {
  AuthContext,
  type AuthContextValue,
} from "./AuthContext";

export function useAuth(): AuthContextValue {
  const context = useContext(AuthContext);

  if (!context) {
    const diagnosticWindow =
      window as typeof window & {
        __easychatbot_auth_context__?: typeof AuthContext;
      };

    console.error(
      "[EasyChatBot Auth] useAuth context lookup FAILED",
      {
        context,
        currentContext: AuthContext,
        registeredContext:
          diagnosticWindow.__easychatbot_auth_context__,
        sameContext:
          diagnosticWindow.__easychatbot_auth_context__ ===
          AuthContext,
        currentUrl: window.location.href,
      },
    );

    throw new Error(
      "useAuth must be used within an AuthProvider.",
    );
  }

  console.log(
    "[EasyChatBot Auth] useAuth context lookup succeeded",
    {
      status: context.status,
    },
  );

  return context;
}

import React from "react";
import ReactDOM from "react-dom/client";
import { BrowserRouter } from "react-router-dom";

import AppRouter from "./router";
import { AuthProvider } from "./auth/AuthProvider";

const rootElement = document.getElementById("root");

if (!rootElement) {
  throw new Error(
    "EasyChatBot React root element was not found.",
  );
}

ReactDOM.createRoot(rootElement).render(
  <React.StrictMode>
    <BrowserRouter>
      <AuthProvider>
        <AppRouter />
      </AuthProvider>
    </BrowserRouter>
  </React.StrictMode>,
);

============================================================
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\frontend\src\router.tsx
============================================================
import {
  Navigate,
  Route,
  Routes,
  useSearchParams,
} from "react-router-dom";

import OperationsCenterGovernancePage from "../app/operations-center/governance/page";
import PrivacyGovernancePage from "../app/privacy-governance/page";
import FunctionalityHealthPage from "../app/operations-center/functionality-health/page";
import EmailWorkflowsPage from "../app/operations-center/email-workflows/page";
import RiskIntelligencePage from "../app/operations-center/compliance/cmp/risk-intelligence/risk-coverage/page";
import WelcomePage from "../app/welcome/page";
import ProjectControlPage from "../app/project-control/page";
import LoginPage from "../app/login/page";

import CinemaPage from "./agentic-cinema/CinemaPage";
import QuestionnaireSelectionPage from "./academy/questionnaires/QuestionnaireSelectionPage";
import ProtectedRoute from "./auth/ProtectedRoute";

function AcademyQuestionnairesRoute() {
  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();

  const worldId =
    searchParams.get("world")?.trim().toLowerCase() ||
    "french";

  return (
    <QuestionnaireSelectionPage
      worldId={worldId}
    />
  );
}

export default function AppRouter() {
  return (
    <Routes>
      {/* =========================================================
          AUTHENTICATION
          ========================================================= */}

      <Route
        path="/login"
        element={<LoginPage />}
      />

      {/* =========================================================
          AGENTIC CINEMA
          Hackathon feature
          Public for current slice
          ========================================================= */}

      <Route
        path="/agentic-cinema"
        element={<CinemaPage />}
      />

      {/* =========================================================
          ACADEMY
          ========================================================= */}

      {/* Academy Welcome */}
      <Route
        path="/welcome"
        element={<WelcomePage />}
      />

      {/* Academy Questionnaire Selection */}
      <Route
        path="/questionnaires"
        element={<AcademyQuestionnairesRoute />}
      />

      {/* =========================================================
          OPERATIONS CENTER
          Protected
          ========================================================= */}

      {/* Operations Center Governance */}
      <Route
        path="/operations-center/governance"
        element={
          <ProtectedRoute>
            <OperationsCenterGovernancePage />
          </ProtectedRoute>
        }
      />

      {/* Operations Center Functionality Health */}
      <Route
        path="/operations-center/functionality-health"
        element={
          <ProtectedRoute>
            <FunctionalityHealthPage />
          </ProtectedRoute>
        }
      />

      {/* Operations Center Email Workflows */}
      <Route
        path="/operations-center/email-workflows"
        element={
          <ProtectedRoute>
            <EmailWorkflowsPage />
          </ProtectedRoute>
        }
      />

      {/* =========================================================
          COMPLIANCE ? CMP ? RISK INTELLIGENCE
          Protected
          ========================================================= */}

      {/* Compliance ? CMP ? Risk Intelligence ? Risk Coverage */}
      <Route
        path="/operations-center/compliance/cmp/risk-intelligence/risk-coverage"
        element={
          <ProtectedRoute>
            <RiskIntelligencePage />
          </ProtectedRoute>
        }
      />

      {/* Backward-compatible redirect for the initial Risk
          Intelligence URL */}
      <Route
        path="/operations-center/risk-intelligence"
        element={
          <Navigate
            to="/operations-center/compliance/cmp/risk-intelligence/risk-coverage"
            replace
          />
        }
      />

      {/* =========================================================
          PRIVACY GOVERNANCE
          Protected
          ========================================================= */}

      {/* Privacy Governance dashboard */}
      <Route
        path="/privacy-governance"
        element={
          <ProtectedRoute>
            <PrivacyGovernancePage />
          </ProtectedRoute>
        }
      />

      {/* Privacy Governance specific case */}
      <Route
        path="/privacy-governance/:caseId"
        element={
          <ProtectedRoute>
            <PrivacyGovernancePage />
          </ProtectedRoute>
        }
      />

      {/* =========================================================
          PROJECT CONTROL
          Protected
          ========================================================= */}

      {/* Project Control Center */}
      <Route
        path="/project-control"
        element={
          <ProtectedRoute>
            <ProjectControlPage />
          </ProtectedRoute>
        }
      />

      {/* =========================================================
          APPLICATION ROOT
          ========================================================= */}

      {/* Application root */}
      <Route
        path="/"
        element={
          <Navigate
            to="/operations-center/governance"
            replace
          />
        }
      />

      {/* =========================================================
          UNKNOWN ROUTES
          ========================================================= */}

      {/* Unknown routes */}
      <Route
        path="*"
        element={
          <Navigate
            to="/operations-center/governance"
            replace
          />
        }
      />
    </Routes>
  );
}

/*
 * =========================================================
 * EasyChatBot Main Dashboard
 * =========================================================
 *
 * Core dashboard structure and presentation.
 *
 * Character-specific visual identity is supplied separately
 * by character-theme.css through CSS variables generated by
 * the backend Character Theme System.
 *
 * This file therefore defines:
 *
 * - dashboard layout
 * - sidebar
 * - navigation
 * - header
 * - cards
 * - companion hero
 * - progress
 * - missions
 * - academy
 * - recommendations
 * - responsive behaviour
 *
 * It does NOT define individual character themes.
 *
 * =========================================================
 */

   FOUNDATION
   ========================================================= */

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\frontend\tests\frontend\auth.spec.tsx
============================================================
import {
  beforeEach,
  describe,
  expect,
  it,
  vi,
} from "vitest";

import {
  fireEvent,
  render,
  screen,
  waitFor,
} from "@testing-library/react";

import {
  MemoryRouter,
  Route,
  Routes,
} from "react-router-dom";

import {
  AuthProvider,
} from "../../src/auth/AuthProvider";

import ProtectedRoute from "../../src/auth/ProtectedRoute";

import LoginPage from "../../app/login/page";


function ProtectedTestPage() {
  return (
    <div>
      Protected content
    </div>
  );
}


function renderProtectedRoute(
  initialEntry = "/protected",
) {
  return render(
    <MemoryRouter
      initialEntries={[initialEntry]}
    >
      <AuthProvider>
        <Routes>
          <Route
            path="/protected"
            element={
              <ProtectedRoute>
                <ProtectedTestPage />
              </ProtectedRoute>
            }
          />

          <Route
            path="/login"
            element={<LoginPage />}
          />
        </Routes>
      </AuthProvider>
    </MemoryRouter>
  );
}


describe("authentication boundary", () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.restoreAllMocks();
    vi.unstubAllGlobals();
  });


  it("redirects an unauthenticated user to login", async () => {
    const fetchMock = vi.fn(
      async (
        input: RequestInfo | URL,
      ) => {
        expect(String(input)).toBe(
          "/auth/session",
        );

        return new Response(
          JSON.stringify({
            detail:
              "Authentication required.",
          }),
          {
            status: 401,
            headers: {
              "Content-Type":
                "application/json",
            },
          },
        );
      },
    );

    vi.stubGlobal(
      "fetch",
      fetchMock,
    );

    renderProtectedRoute();

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(
        screen.getByRole("heading", {
          name: "EasyChatBot",
        }),
      ).toBeInTheDocument();
    });

    expect(
      screen.getByText(
        "Authorized access required.",
      ),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();

    expect(
      screen.queryByText(
        "Protected content",
      ),
    ).not.toBeInTheDocument();

    expect(
      fetchMock,
    ).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      "/auth/session",
      expect.objectContaining({
        method: "GET",
        credentials: "include",
      }),
    );
  });


  it("keeps an authenticated user on the protected route", async () => {
    const fetchMock = vi.fn(
      async () => {
        return new Response(
          JSON.stringify({
            authenticated: true,
          }),
          {
            status: 200,
            headers: {
              "Content-Type":
                "application/json",
            },
          },
        );
      },
    );

    vi.stubGlobal(
      "fetch",
      fetchMock,
    );

    renderProtectedRoute();

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(
        screen.getByText(
          "Protected content",
        ),
      ).toBeInTheDocument();
    });

    expect(
      screen.queryByRole("heading", {
        name: "EasyChatBot",
      }),
    ).not.toBeInTheDocument();

    expect(
      fetchMock,
    ).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      "/auth/session",
      expect.objectContaining({
        method: "GET",
        credentials: "include",
      }),
    );
  });


  it("fails closed when the session check has an unexpected error", async () => {
    const fetchMock = vi.fn(
      async () => {
        return new Response(
          JSON.stringify({
            detail:
              "Internal Server Error",
          }),
          {
            status: 500,
            headers: {
              "Content-Type":
                "application/json",
            },
          },
        );
      },
    );

    vi.stubGlobal(
      "fetch",
      fetchMock,
    );

    renderProtectedRoute();

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(
        screen.getByRole("heading", {
          name: "EasyChatBot",
        }),
      ).toBeInTheDocument();
    });

    expect(
      screen.getByText(
        "Authorized access required.",
      ),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();

    expect(
      screen.queryByText(
        "Protected content",
      ),
    ).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });


  it("submits valid credentials through the existing auth API", async () => {
    const fetchMock = vi.fn(
      async (
        input: RequestInfo | URL,
        init?: RequestInit,
      ) => {
        const url = String(input);

        if (
          url === "/auth/login" &&
          init?.method === "POST"
        ) {
          return new Response(
            JSON.stringify({
              detail:
                "Login successful.",
              expires_at:
                "2026-09-12T09:00:00Z",
            }),
            {
              status: 200,
              headers: {
                "Content-Type":
                  "application/json",
              },
            },
          );
        }

        return new Response(
          JSON.stringify({
            authenticated: false,
          }),
          {
            status: 401,
            headers: {
              "Content-Type":
                "application/json",
            },
          },
        );
      },
    );

    vi.stubGlobal(
      "fetch",
      fetchMock,
    );

    render(
      <MemoryRouter
        initialEntries={[
          "/login?redirect=%2Fproject-control",
        ]}
      >
        <AuthProvider>
          <Routes>
            <Route
              path="/login"
              element={<LoginPage />}
            />

            <Route
              path="/project-control"
              element={
                <div>
                  Project Control
                </div>
              }
            />
          </Routes>
        </AuthProvider>
      </MemoryRouter>,
    );

    fireEvent.change(
      screen.getByLabelText("Email"),
      {
        target: {
          value:
            "authorized@example.com",
        },
      },
    );

    fireEvent.change(
      screen.getByLabelText("Password"),
      {
        target: {
          value: "test-password",
        },
      },
    );

    fireEvent.click(
      screen.getByRole("button", {
        name: "Sign in",
      }),
    );

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(
        screen.getByText(
          "Project Control",
        ),
      ).toBeInTheDocument();
    });

    expect(
      fetchMock,
    ).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      "/auth/login",
      expect.objectContaining({
        method: "POST",
        credentials: "include",
      }),
    );
  });


  it("does not expose authentication credentials in browser storage", async () => {
    const localStorageSetItem =
      vi.spyOn(
        Storage.prototype,
        "setItem",
      );

    const sessionStorageSetItem =
      vi.spyOn(
        Storage.prototype,
        "setItem",
      );

    vi.stubGlobal(
      "fetch",
      vi.fn(async () => {
        return new Response(
          JSON.stringify({
            detail:
              "Login successful.",
            expires_at:
              "2026-09-12T09:00:00Z",
          }),
          {
            status: 200,
            headers: {
              "Content-Type":
                "application/json",
            },
          },
        );
      }),
    );

    render(
      <MemoryRouter
        initialEntries={["/login"]}
      >
        <AuthProvider>
          <Routes>
            <Route
              path="/login"
              element={<LoginPage />}
            />

            <Route
              path="/project-control"
              element={
                <div>
                  Project Control
                </div>
              }
            />
          </Routes>
        </AuthProvider>
      </MemoryRouter>,
    );

    fireEvent.change(
      screen.getByLabelText("Email"),
      {
        target: {
          value:
            "authorized@example.com",
        },
      },
    );

    fireEvent.change(
      screen.getByLabelText("Password"),
      {
        target: {
          value: "secret-password",
        },
      },
    );

    fireEvent.click(
      screen.getByRole("button", {
        name: "Sign in",
      }),
    );

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(
        screen.queryByText(
          "Authorized access required.",
        ),
      ).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    });

    expect(
      localStorageSetItem,
    ).not.toHaveBeenCalled();

    expect(
      sessionStorageSetItem,
    ).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});


============================================================
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\frontend\tests\frontend\functionality-health.spec.tsx
============================================================
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

import {
  fireEvent,
  render,
  screen,
  waitFor,
  within,
} from "@testing-library/react";

import FunctionalityHealthDashboard from "../../app/components/operations-center/functionality-health/FunctionalityHealthDashboard";
import {
  resumeFunctionality,
  stopFunctionality,
} from "../../app/lib/api/functionalityHealthControls";

const healthPayload = {
  generated_at: "2026-09-05T08:00:00Z",
  environment: "adults" as const,
  platform_health: "healthy" as const,
  platform_health_score: 96,
  user_impact: "LOW" as const,
  counts: {
    healthy: 11,
    degraded: 2,
    incident: 1,
    maintenance: 0,
    disabled: 0,
    unknown: 0,
  },
  functionalities: [
    {
      functionality_id: "conversation",
      name: "AI Conversation",
      category: "AI",
      description: "Core AI conversation capability.",
      status: "healthy" as const,
      health_score: 98,
      latency_ms: 240,
      provider: "openai",
      model: "gpt-5-mini",
      capability: "conversation",
      checked_at: "2026-09-05T08:00:00Z",
      controllable: true,
      risk_level: "low",
      reason: "Operating normally.",
    },
    {
      functionality_id: "voice_tts",
      name: "Voice / TTS",
      category: "AI",
      description: "Text-to-speech capability.",
      status: "degraded" as const,
      health_score: 72,
      latency_ms: 1800,
      provider: "openai",
      model: "tts",
      capability: "voice_tts",
      checked_at: "2026-09-05T08:00:00Z",
      controllable: true,
      risk_level: "medium",
      reason: "Provider latency above threshold.",
    },
  ],
  attention: [],
};

describe("Functionality Health", () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.restoreAllMocks();

    vi.spyOn(globalThis, "fetch").mockResolvedValue(
      new Response(JSON.stringify(healthPayload), {
        status: 200,
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
        },
      }),
    );
  });

  it("renders the professional Functionality Health dashboard", async () => {
    render(<FunctionalityHealthDashboard />);

    expect(
      await screen.findByRole("heading", {
        name: "Functionality Health",
      }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();

    expect(
      screen.getByText(
        "Real-time operational health across all EasyChatBot capabilities.",
      ),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();

    expect(screen.getByText("Platform Health")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText("Functionality Status")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText("Status by Category")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText("Functionality Overview")).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("renders live health data from the API", async () => {
    render(<FunctionalityHealthDashboard />);

    await screen.findByRole("heading", {
      name: "Functionality Health",
    });

    expect(screen.getByText("96%")).toBeInTheDocument();

    const table = screen.getByRole("table");

    expect(
      within(table).getByText("AI Conversation"),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();

    expect(
      within(table).getByText("Voice / TTS"),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();

    expect(within(table).getByText("98%")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(table).getByText("72%")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(table).getByText("240 ms")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(table).getByText("1800 ms")).toBeInTheDocument();

    const conversationRow = within(table).getByRole("row", {
      name: /AI Conversation.*AI.*Healthy.*98%.*240 ms/i,
    });

    const voiceRow = within(table).getByRole("row", {
      name: /Voice \/ TTS.*AI.*Degraded.*72%.*1800 ms/i,
    });

    expect(
      within(conversationRow).getByText("openai"),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();

    expect(
      within(voiceRow).getByText("openai"),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("allows an operator to inspect a functionality by selecting its row", async () => {
    render(<FunctionalityHealthDashboard />);

    await screen.findByRole("heading", {
      name: "Functionality Health",
    });

    const table = screen.getByRole("table");

    const voiceRow = within(table).getByRole("row", {
      name: /Voice \/ TTS.*AI.*Degraded.*72%.*1800 ms/i,
    });

    fireEvent.click(voiceRow);

    const detailsPanel = await screen.findByRole("complementary");

    expect(
      within(detailsPanel).getByRole("heading", {
        name: "Voice / TTS",
      }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();

    expect(
      within(detailsPanel).getByText("Text-to-speech capability."),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();

    expect(
      within(detailsPanel).getByText("voice_tts"),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();

    expect(
      within(detailsPanel).getByText("tts"),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();

    expect(
      within(detailsPanel).getByText(
        "Provider latency above threshold.",
      ),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("refreshes the health data when Refresh is clicked", async () => {
    const fetchSpy = vi.spyOn(globalThis, "fetch").mockResolvedValue(
      new Response(JSON.stringify(healthPayload), {
        status: 200,
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
        },
      }),
    );

    render(<FunctionalityHealthDashboard />);

    await screen.findByRole("heading", {
      name: "Functionality Health",
    });

    const initialCallCount = fetchSpy.mock.calls.length;

    fireEvent.click(
      screen.getByRole("button", {
        name: "Refresh",
      }),
    );

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(fetchSpy.mock.calls.length).toBeGreaterThan(
        initialCallCount,
      );
    });

    expect(fetchSpy).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith(
      "/api/operations-center/functionality-health?environment=adults",
      {
        headers: {
          Accept: "application/json",
        },
      },
    );
  });

  it("shows an accessible error when the health API fails", async () => {
    vi.restoreAllMocks();

    vi.spyOn(globalThis, "fetch").mockResolvedValue(
      new Response("Service unavailable", {
        status: 503,
      }),
    );

    render(<FunctionalityHealthDashboard />);

    expect(
      await screen.findByRole("alert"),
    ).toHaveTextContent(
      "Functionality health API failed: 503",
    );
  });

  it("shows the backend-authorised stop control and requires a reason", async () => {
    const stopSpy = vi
      .spyOn(
        await import("../../app/lib/api/functionalityHealthControls"),
        "stopFunctionality",
      )
      .mockResolvedValue({
        functionality_id: "conversation",
        environment: "adults",
        availability: "disabled",
        action: "stop",
        reason: "Operational incident",
        changed_at: "2026-09-05T08:05:00Z",
        changed_by: "operator",
      });

    render(<FunctionalityHealthDashboard />);

    await screen.findByRole("heading", {
      name: "Functionality Health",
    });

    fireEvent.click(
      screen.getByRole("row", {
        name: /AI Conversation.*AI.*Healthy.*98%.*240 ms/i,
      }),
    );

    const detailsPanel = await screen.findByRole("complementary");

    fireEvent.click(
      within(detailsPanel).getByRole("button", {
        name: "Stop functionality",
      }),
    );

    expect(
      within(detailsPanel).getByRole("textbox", {
        name: /Reason/i,
      }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();

    expect(
      within(detailsPanel).getByRole("button", {
        name: "Confirm Stop",
      }),
    ).toBeDisabled();

    fireEvent.change(
      within(detailsPanel).getByRole("textbox", {
        name: /Reason/i,
      }),
      {
        target: {
          value: "Operational incident",
        },
      },
    );

    expect(
      within(detailsPanel).getByRole("button", {
        name: "Confirm Stop",
      }),
    ).toBeEnabled();

    fireEvent.click(
      within(detailsPanel).getByRole("button", {
        name: "Confirm Stop",
      }),
    );

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(stopSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        "conversation",
        "adults",
        "Operational incident",
      );
    });
  });

  it("does not allow an empty stop reason", async () => {
    render(<FunctionalityHealthDashboard />);

    await screen.findByRole("heading", {
      name: "Functionality Health",
    });

    fireEvent.click(
      screen.getByRole("row", {
        name: /AI Conversation.*AI.*Healthy.*98%.*240 ms/i,
      }),
    );

    const detailsPanel = await screen.findByRole("complementary");

    fireEvent.click(
      within(detailsPanel).getByRole("button", {
        name: "Stop functionality",
      }),
    );

    expect(
      within(detailsPanel).getByRole("button", {
        name: "Confirm Stop",
      }),
    ).toBeDisabled();
  });

  it("shows a safe error when a stop is rejected", async () => {
    vi.spyOn(
      await import("../../app/lib/api/functionalityHealthControls"),
      "stopFunctionality",
    ).mockRejectedValue(new Error("Permission denied"));

    render(<FunctionalityHealthDashboard />);

    await screen.findByRole("heading", {
      name: "Functionality Health",
    });

    fireEvent.click(
      screen.getByRole("row", {
        name: /AI Conversation.*AI.*Healthy.*98%.*240 ms/i,
      }),
    );

    const detailsPanel = await screen.findByRole("complementary");

    fireEvent.click(
      within(detailsPanel).getByRole("button", {
        name: "Stop functionality",
      }),
    );

    fireEvent.change(
      within(detailsPanel).getByRole("textbox", {
        name: /Reason/i,
      }),
      {
        target: {
          value: "Operational incident",
        },
      },
    );

    fireEvent.click(
      within(detailsPanel).getByRole("button", {
        name: "Confirm Stop",
      }),
    );

    expect(
      await within(detailsPanel).findByRole("alert"),
    ).toHaveTextContent("Permission denied");
  });

  it("uses the resume control when the backend reports the functionality disabled", async () => {
    const disabledPayload = {
      ...healthPayload,
      functionalities: healthPayload.functionalities.map((item) =>
        item.functionality_id === "conversation"
          ? {
              ...item,
              availability: "disabled" as const,
            }
          : item,
      ),
    };

    vi.restoreAllMocks();

    vi.spyOn(globalThis, "fetch").mockResolvedValue(
      new Response(JSON.stringify(disabledPayload), {
        status: 200,
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
        },
      }),
    );

    const resumeSpy = vi
      .spyOn(
        await import("../../app/lib/api/functionalityHealthControls"),
        "resumeFunctionality",
      )
      .mockResolvedValue({
        functionality_id: "conversation",
        environment: "adults",
        availability: "enabled",
        action: "resume",
        reason: null,
        changed_at: "2026-09-05T08:05:00Z",
        changed_by: "operator",
      });

    render(<FunctionalityHealthDashboard />);

    await screen.findByRole("heading", {
      name: "Functionality Health",
    });

    fireEvent.click(
      screen.getByRole("row", {
        name: /AI Conversation.*AI.*Healthy.*98%.*240 ms/i,
      }),
    );

    const detailsPanel = await screen.findByRole("complementary");

    fireEvent.click(
      within(detailsPanel).getByRole("button", {
        name: "Resume functionality",
      }),
    );

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(resumeSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        "conversation",
        "adults",
      );
    });
  });

  it("uses backend-authorised operational controls instead of the M1 read-only notice", async () => {
    render(<FunctionalityHealthDashboard />);

    await screen.findByRole("heading", {
      name: "Functionality Health",
    });

    expect(
      screen.queryByText(
        "M1 is read-only. Operational actions will be introduced through backend-authorised controls with confirmation and audit logging.",
      ),
    ).not.toBeInTheDocument();

    expect(
      screen.getByText("Backend-authorised operational controls"),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
