import "@testing-library/jest-dom/vitest";

import {
  fireEvent,
  render,
  screen,
  waitFor,
} from "@testing-library/react";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

import ProjectControlPage from "../../app/project-control/page";

const snapshot = {
  project_id: "easychatbot",
  project_name: "EasyChatBot",
  project_status: "on_track",
  overall_progress_percent: 85,
  current_milestone: "Beta 1",
  current_workstream: "Project Control Center",
  release_readiness: "BETA READY",
  open_blockers: 0,
  open_risks: 0,
  workstreams: [
    {
      workstream_id: "pcd",
      name: "Project Control Center",
      completion_percent: 85,
      status: "healthy",
      source: "api-test",
      updated_at: null,
    },
  ],
  metrics: [],
  issues: [],
  risks: [],
  actions: [],
  milestones: [],
  sources: [
    {
      source_id: "api-test",
      name: "API Test Source",
      status: "available",
      updated_at: null,
      error: null,
    },
  ],
  generated_at:
    "2026-09-08T15:00:00+00:00",
};

describe(
  "Project Control Center",
  () => {
    it(
      "renders the authoritative project snapshot",
      async () => {
        const fetchMock =
          vi
            .spyOn(globalThis, "fetch")
            .mockResolvedValue(
              new Response(
                JSON.stringify(snapshot),
                {
                  status: 200,
                  headers: {
                    "Content-Type":
                      "application/json",
                  },
                },
              ),
            );

        render(
          <ProjectControlPage />,
        );

        expect(
          screen.getByRole(
            "status",
          ),
        ).toHaveTextContent(
          "Loading project control data",
        );

        await waitFor(() => {
          expect(
            screen.getByText(
              "EasyChatBot",
            ),
          ).toBeInTheDocument();
        });

        expect(
          screen.getByText(
            "ON TRACK",
          ),
        ).toBeInTheDocument();

        expect(
          screen.getByText(
            "85.0%",
          ),
        ).toBeInTheDocument();

        expect(
          screen.getByText(
            "BETA READY",
          ),
        ).toBeInTheDocument();

        expect(
          fetchMock,
        ).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
          "/project-control/projects/easychatbot",
          expect.objectContaining({
            method: "GET",
            credentials: "include",
          }),
        );

        fetchMock.mockRestore();
      },
    );

    it(
      "shows an API failure and provides retry",
      async () => {
        const fetchMock =
          vi
            .spyOn(globalThis, "fetch")
            .mockRejectedValue(
              new Error(
                "Network failure",
              ),
            );

        render(
          <ProjectControlPage />,
        );

        await waitFor(() => {
          expect(
            screen.getByRole(
              "alert",
            ),
          ).toBeInTheDocument();
        });

        expect(
          screen.getByText(
            "Unable to load Project Control data.",
          ),
        ).toBeInTheDocument();

        const retryButton =
          screen.getByRole(
            "button",
            {
              name: "Retry",
            },
          );

        fireEvent.click(
          retryButton,
        );

        await waitFor(() => {
          expect(
            fetchMock,
          ).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);

          expect(
            screen.getByRole(
              "alert",
            ),
          ).toHaveTextContent(
            "Unable to load Project Control data.",
          );
        });

        fetchMock.mockRestore();
      },
    );

    it(
      "shows unavailable source information",
      async () => {
        const degradedSnapshot = {
          ...snapshot,
          sources: [
            {
              ...snapshot.sources[0],
              status: "unavailable",
              error:
                "Source unavailable",
            },
          ],
        };

        const fetchMock =
          vi
            .spyOn(globalThis, "fetch")
            .mockResolvedValue(
              new Response(
                JSON.stringify(
                  degradedSnapshot,
                ),
                {
                  status: 200,
                  headers: {
                    "Content-Type":
                      "application/json",
                  },
                },
              ),
            );

        render(
          <ProjectControlPage />,
        );

        const warning =
          await screen.findByRole(
            "status",
            {
              name: "Source warning",
            },
          );

        expect(
          warning,
        ).toHaveTextContent(
          "Some project-control sources are unavailable or stale.",
        );

        const sourceName =
          screen.getByText(
            "API Test Source",
          );

        const sourceRow =
          sourceName.closest("li");

        if (!sourceRow) {
          throw new Error(
            "Expected API Test Source to be rendered inside a list item.",
          );
        }

        expect(
          sourceRow,
        ).toHaveTextContent(
          "Unavailable",
        );

        expect(
          sourceRow,
        ).toHaveTextContent(
          "Source unavailable",
        );

        fetchMock.mockRestore();
      },
    );
  },
);

import { render, screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react";
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import RiskIntelligenceDashboard from "../../app/operations-center/risk-intelligence/RiskIntelligenceDashboard";
import { getRiskIntelligence } from "../../app/lib/api/riskIntelligence";

vi.mock("../../app/lib/api/riskIntelligence", () => ({
  getRiskIntelligence: vi.fn(),
}));

const mockedGetRiskIntelligence = vi.mocked(getRiskIntelligence);

describe("RiskIntelligenceDashboard", () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.clearAllMocks();
  });

  it("renders authoritative available metrics", async () => {
    mockedGetRiskIntelligence.mockResolvedValue({
      lifecycle_id: "CMP-2026-2028",
      as_of: "2026-09-06T12:00:00Z",
      population_status: "AVAILABLE",
      total_item_years: {
        value: 44,
        availability: "AVAILABLE",
      },
      distinct_controls: {
        value: 17,
        availability: "AVAILABLE",
      },
      distinct_domains: {
        value: 16,
        availability: "AVAILABLE",
      },
      yearly_population: {
        2026: 10,
        2027: 17,
        2028: 17,
      },
      risk_distribution: {
        HIGH: 41,
        MEDIUM: 2,
        LOW: 1,
      },
      domain_distribution: {
        "AI Governance": 6,
        "Access Control": 3,
      },
      source: "CMPMonitoringPlanItemModel + CMPControlModel",
    });

    render(<RiskIntelligenceDashboard />);

    expect(
      screen.getByText("Loading Risk Intelligence�"),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.getByText("44")).toBeInTheDocument();
    });

    expect(screen.getByText("17")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText("16")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText("2026: 10")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText("HIGH: 41")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText("AI Governance: 6")).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("does not convert unavailable metrics into zero", async () => {
    mockedGetRiskIntelligence.mockResolvedValue({
      lifecycle_id: "CMP-2026-2028",
      as_of: "2026-09-06T12:00:00Z",
      population_status: "NOT_AVAILABLE",
      total_item_years: {
        value: null,
        availability: "NOT_AVAILABLE",
      },
      distinct_controls: {
        value: null,
        availability: "NOT_AVAILABLE",
      },
      distinct_domains: {
        value: null,
        availability: "NOT_AVAILABLE",
      },
      yearly_population: {},
      risk_distribution: {},
      domain_distribution: {},
      source: "CMPMonitoringPlanItemModel + CMPControlModel",
    });

    render(<RiskIntelligenceDashboard />);

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.getAllByText("Not available").length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    });

    expect(screen.queryByText("0")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("shows a retry action when the API fails", async () => {
    mockedGetRiskIntelligence.mockRejectedValue(
      new Error("Risk Intelligence is temporarily unavailable."),
    );

    render(<RiskIntelligenceDashboard />);

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(
        screen.getByRole("alert"),
      ).toBeInTheDocument();
    });

    expect(
      screen.getByText("Risk Intelligence is temporarily unavailable."),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();

    expect(
      screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Retry" }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

/// <reference types="vitest/config" />

import { defineConfig } from "vite";
import react from "@vitejs/plugin-react";
import { dirname, resolve } from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const __dirname = dirname(__filename);

export default defineConfig({
  plugins: [
    react(),
  ],

  // =====================================================
  // Vitest
  // =====================================================

  test: {
    environment: "jsdom",
    globals: true,

    include: [
      "./tests/frontend/**/*.{test,spec}.{ts,tsx}",
    ],

    setupFiles: [
      "./vitest.setup.ts",
    ],
  },

  // =====================================================
  // Module resolution
  // =====================================================

  resolve: {
    alias: {
      "@": resolve(__dirname, "."),

      react: resolve(
        __dirname,
        "node_modules/react",
      ),

      "react-dom": resolve(
        __dirname,
        "node_modules/react-dom",
      ),

      "@testing-library/react": resolve(
        __dirname,
        "node_modules/@testing-library/react",
      ),

      "@testing-library/jest-dom": resolve(
        __dirname,
        "node_modules/@testing-library/jest-dom",
      ),
    },
  },

  // =====================================================
  // Vite development server
  // =====================================================

  server: {
    host: "127.0.0.1",
    port: 5173,

    fs: {
      allow: [
        "..",
      ],
    },

    proxy: {
      // =================================================
      // Authentication API
      //
      // IMPORTANT:
      // The browser never receives or stores the session ID.
      //
      // The existing backend authentication service creates
      // the HttpOnly easychatbot_session cookie.
      // =================================================

      "/auth": {
        target: "http://127.0.0.1:8000",
        changeOrigin: true,
      },

      // =================================================
      // Project Control API
      //
      // IMPORTANT:
      // Do NOT proxy /project-control itself.
      //
      // /project-control is a React application route.
      //
      // The API client uses:
      // /project-control/projects/{project_id}
      //
      // Therefore only the API resource path is proxied.
      // =================================================

      "/project-control/projects": {
        target: "http://127.0.0.1:8000",
        changeOrigin: true,
      },

      // =================================================
      // Existing EasyChatBot backend APIs
      // =================================================

      "/operations/governance": {
        target: "http://127.0.0.1:8000",
        changeOrigin: true,
      },

      "/operations-center/dashboard": {
        target: "http://127.0.0.1:8000",
        changeOrigin: true,
      },

      "/controls": {
        target: "http://127.0.0.1:8000",
        changeOrigin: true,
      },

      "/governance": {
        target: "http://127.0.0.1:8000",
        changeOrigin: true,
      },

      // =================================================
      // API boundary
      //
      // Includes:
      //
      // /api/privacy/governance/summary
      // /api/privacy/governance/status-chart
      // /api/privacy/governance/cases
      // /api/privacy/governance/cases/{case_id}
      // /api/operations-center/functionality-health
      // /api/operations-center/risk-intelligence
      //
      // =================================================

      "/api": {
        target: "http://127.0.0.1:8000",
        changeOrigin: true,
      },
    },
  },
});

import React from "react";
import ReactDOM from "react-dom/client";
import { BrowserRouter } from "react-router-dom";

import AppRouter from "./src/router";
import { AuthProvider } from "./src/auth/AuthProvider";

import "./styles/operations-center.css";

const rootElement = document.getElementById("root");

if (!rootElement) {
    throw new Error(
        "EasyChatBot React root element was not found.",
    );
}

ReactDOM.createRoot(rootElement).render(
    <React.StrictMode>
        <BrowserRouter>
            <AuthProvider>
                <AppRouter />
            </AuthProvider>
        </BrowserRouter>
    </React.StrictMode>,
);

import React from "react";
import ReactDOM from "react-dom/client";
import { BrowserRouter } from "react-router-dom";

import AppRouter from "./router";
import { AuthProvider } from "./auth/AuthProvider";

const rootElement = document.getElementById("root");

if (!rootElement) {
  throw new Error(
    "EasyChatBot React root element was not found.",
  );
}

ReactDOM.createRoot(rootElement).render(
  <React.StrictMode>
    <BrowserRouter>
      <AuthProvider>
        <AppRouter />
      </AuthProvider>
    </BrowserRouter>
  </React.StrictMode>,
);

import {
  Navigate,
  Route,
  Routes,
  useSearchParams,
} from "react-router-dom";

import OperationsCenterGovernancePage from "../app/operations-center/governance/page";
import PrivacyGovernancePage from "../app/privacy-governance/page";
import FunctionalityHealthPage from "../app/operations-center/functionality-health/page";
import EmailWorkflowsPage from "../app/operations-center/email-workflows/page";
import RiskIntelligencePage from "../app/operations-center/compliance/cmp/risk-intelligence/risk-coverage/page";
import WelcomePage from "../app/welcome/page";
import ProjectControlPage from "../app/project-control/page";
import LoginPage from "../app/login/page";

import CinemaPage from "./agentic-cinema/CinemaPage";
import QuestionnaireSelectionPage from "./academy/questionnaires/QuestionnaireSelectionPage";
import ProtectedRoute from "./auth/ProtectedRoute";

function AcademyQuestionnairesRoute() {
  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();

  const worldId =
    searchParams.get("world")?.trim().toLowerCase() ||
    "french";

  return (
    <QuestionnaireSelectionPage
      worldId={worldId}
    />
  );
}

export default function AppRouter() {
  return (
    <Routes>
      {/* =========================================================
          AUTHENTICATION
          ========================================================= */}

      <Route
        path="/login"
        element={<LoginPage />}
      />

      {/* =========================================================
          AGENTIC CINEMA
          Hackathon feature
          Public for current slice
          ========================================================= */}

      <Route
        path="/agentic-cinema"
        element={<CinemaPage />}
      />

      {/* =========================================================
          ACADEMY
          ========================================================= */}

      {/* Academy Welcome */}
      <Route
        path="/welcome"
        element={<WelcomePage />}
      />

      {/* Academy Questionnaire Selection */}
      <Route
        path="/questionnaires"
        element={<AcademyQuestionnairesRoute />}
      />

      {/* =========================================================
          OPERATIONS CENTER
          Protected
          ========================================================= */}

      {/* Operations Center Governance */}
      <Route
        path="/operations-center/governance"
        element={
          <ProtectedRoute>
            <OperationsCenterGovernancePage />
          </ProtectedRoute>
        }
      />

      {/* Operations Center Functionality Health */}
      <Route
        path="/operations-center/functionality-health"
        element={
          <ProtectedRoute>
            <FunctionalityHealthPage />
          </ProtectedRoute>
        }
      />

      {/* Operations Center Email Workflows */}
      <Route
        path="/operations-center/email-workflows"
        element={
          <ProtectedRoute>
            <EmailWorkflowsPage />
          </ProtectedRoute>
        }
      />

      {/* =========================================================
          COMPLIANCE ? CMP ? RISK INTELLIGENCE
          Protected
          ========================================================= */}

      {/* Compliance ? CMP ? Risk Intelligence ? Risk Coverage */}
      <Route
        path="/operations-center/compliance/cmp/risk-intelligence/risk-coverage"
        element={
          <ProtectedRoute>
            <RiskIntelligencePage />
          </ProtectedRoute>
        }
      />

      {/* Backward-compatible redirect for the initial Risk
          Intelligence URL */}
      <Route
        path="/operations-center/risk-intelligence"
        element={
          <Navigate
            to="/operations-center/compliance/cmp/risk-intelligence/risk-coverage"
            replace
          />
        }
      />

      {/* =========================================================
          PRIVACY GOVERNANCE
          Protected
          ========================================================= */}

      {/* Privacy Governance dashboard */}
      <Route
        path="/privacy-governance"
        element={
          <ProtectedRoute>
            <PrivacyGovernancePage />
          </ProtectedRoute>
        }
      />

      {/* Privacy Governance specific case */}
      <Route
        path="/privacy-governance/:caseId"
        element={
          <ProtectedRoute>
            <PrivacyGovernancePage />
          </ProtectedRoute>
        }
      />

      {/* =========================================================
          PROJECT CONTROL
          Protected
          ========================================================= */}

      {/* Project Control Center */}
      <Route
        path="/project-control"
        element={
          <ProtectedRoute>
            <ProjectControlPage />
          </ProtectedRoute>
        }
      />

      {/* =========================================================
          APPLICATION ROOT
          ========================================================= */}

      {/* Application root */}
      <Route
        path="/"
        element={
          <Navigate
            to="/operations-center/governance"
            replace
          />
        }
      />

      {/* =========================================================
          UNKNOWN ROUTES
          ========================================================= */}

      {/* Unknown routes */}
      <Route
        path="*"
        element={
          <Navigate
            to="/operations-center/governance"
            replace
          />
        }
      />
    </Routes>
  );
}
